<!-- Bandwise — Learn -->
<!DOCTYPE html><html class="light" lang="en"><head>
<meta charset="utf-8">

<title>Bandwise - Learn Path</title>

            "outline": "#787585",
            "tertiary-fixed": "#9ff2e3",
            "on-secondary-fixed": "#141f00",
            "primary-fixed": "#e4dfff",
            "secondary": "#516527",
            "secondary-container": "#d3ec9e",
            "on-tertiary-fixed-variant": "#005047",

<style>
    .material-symbols-outlined {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 0, 'wght' 500, 'GRAD' 0, 'opsz' 24;
      display: inline-block;
      vertical-align: middle;
      line-height: 1;
    }
    .material-symbols-outlined.fill-icon {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1, 'wght' 600, 'GRAD' 0, 'opsz' 24;
    }
    @keyframes subtle-pulse {
      0%, 100% { transform: scale(1); box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(221, 246, 167, 0.7); }
      50% { transform: scale(1.05); box-shadow: 0 0 0 12px rgba(221, 246, 167, 0); }
    }
    .pulse-glow {
      animation: subtle-pulse 2.2s infinite ease-in-out;
    }
    .ambient-card {
      box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(37, 36, 58, 0.06);
      border: 1px solid rgba(37, 36, 58, 0.04);
    }
  </style>
<style>
    body {
      min-height: max(884px, 100dvh);
    }
  </style>
  </head>

<!-- Fixed Container Wrapper for Mobile (390px spec max viewport) -->
<div class="w-full max-w-[390px] mx-auto min-h-screen flex flex-col relative bg-[#F7F6FC] pb-28">
<!-- TopAppBar Component (from Shared Components JSON) -->
<header class="fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-40 flex justify-between items-center px-margin h-16 max-w-[390px] mx-auto bg-[#F7F6FC] dark:bg-inverse-surface shadow-sm dark:shadow-none">
<!-- Leading: Avatar -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-surface-container-high border-2 border-primary-container/20 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary fill-icon" data-icon="person">person</span>
</div>
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm font-bold text-primary dark:text-inverse-primary tracking-tight">Bandwise</span>
</div>
<!-- Trailing: Streak Indicator & Icon -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-secondary-fixed text-on-secondary-fixed px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full shadow-xs">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill-icon text-on-secondary-fixed text-[18px]" data-icon="local_fire_department" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">local_fire_department</span>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md">14</span>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Area: Padding for TopAppBar -->
<main class="flex-1 px-margin pt-20">
<!-- Unit Selector Header & Badge -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5 mb-4">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#FEF4EF] text-[#F69E7B] font-label-sm text-label-sm uppercase tracking-wide font-semibold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" data-icon="edit_note">edit_note</span>
            Writing Academic
          </span>
<button class="flex items-center gap-1 text-primary font-label-md text-label-md hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity">
<span class="">Change Unit</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]" data-icon="expand_more">expand_more</span>
</button>
</div>
<h1 class="text-headline-lg-mobile font-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface">
          Unit 3: Academic Task 1 Mastery
        </h1>
</div>
<!-- Unit Progress Card (18px white card, soft shadow, lime highlight) -->
<div class="ambient-card bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 mb-8 text-left">
<div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-2.5">
<div>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
<span class="text-headline-md font-headline-md text-on-surface font-bold">78%</span>
<span class="text-label-md font-label-md text-on-surface-variant">Completed</span>
</div>
<p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Next milestone: <strong class="text-primary font-semibold">Advanced Mastery</strong></p>
</div>
<!-- Projected Badge Pill -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 bg-[#EBF8F1] rounded-full border border-[#38A169]/30"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[#38A169] text-[15px] fill-icon" data-icon="verified" style="font-variation-settings: &quot;FILL&quot; 1;">verified</span><span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-[#38A169] font-bold">Accuracy 88%</span></div>
</div>
<!-- Progress Bar (Lavender base, Lime #DDF6A7 highlight) -->
<div class="w-full h-3 bg-surface-container rounded-full overflow-hidden p-0.5">
<div class="h-full bg-secondary-fixed rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ease-out flex items-center justify-end pr-1" style="width: 78%;">
<div class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-on-secondary-fixed"></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Content - Vertical Learning Path -->
<div class="relative py-2">
<!-- Continuous Winding SVG Connector Line -->
<div class="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none z-0 flex justify-center">
<svg class="w-full h-full" fill="none" preserveAspectRatio="none" viewBox="0 0 340 780">
<!-- Background Path Track -->
<path d="M 70,30 C 70,90 270,110 270,165 C 270,225 70,245 70,305 C 70,365 270,385 270,450 C 270,515 70,535 70,595 C 70,660 170,670 170,720" stroke="#E9E5FF" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="6"></path>
<!-- Completed Progress Segment -->
<path d="M 70,30 C 70,90 270,110 270,165 C 270,225 70,245 70,305 C 70,365 270,385 270,450" stroke="#6558D3" stroke-dasharray="8 4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="6"></path>
</svg>
</div>
<!-- Nodes Vertical Container -->
<div class="relative z-10 flex flex-col gap-10">
<!-- Node 1: Completed (Left Aligned) -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-4 text-left">
<div class="relative flex-shrink-0 ml-2">
<div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center shadow-md border-4 border-surface-container-highest cursor-pointer active:scale-95 transition-transform">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[28px] fill-icon font-bold" data-icon="check" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest ambient-card rounded-[18px] p-3.5 flex-1 text-left">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-0.5"><span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-[#38A169] bg-[#EBF8F1] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-[12px] flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[15px] fill-icon text-[#38A169]" data-icon="check_circle" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check_circle</span>Mastered</span></div>
<h3 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface text-[15px] leading-tight mt-1">Interpreting Bar &amp; Line Graphs</h3>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Node 2: Completed (Right Aligned) -->
<div class="flex items-center flex-row-reverse gap-4 text-left">
<div class="relative flex-shrink-0 mr-2">
<div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center shadow-md border-4 border-surface-container-highest cursor-pointer active:scale-95 transition-transform">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[28px] fill-icon font-bold" data-icon="check" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest ambient-card rounded-[18px] p-3.5 flex-1 text-left">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between flex-row-reverse mb-0.5"><span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-[#38A169] bg-[#EBF8F1] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-[12px] flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[15px] fill-icon text-[#38A169]" data-icon="check_circle" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check_circle</span>Mastered</span></div>
<h3 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface text-[15px] leading-tight mt-1">Overview Paragraph Techniques</h3>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Node 3: Needs Review (Left Aligned) with Semantic Warning tokens -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-4 text-left">
<div class="relative flex-shrink-0 ml-2">
<div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-[#FEF6EB] border-4 border-[#E29A36] flex items-center justify-center shadow-md cursor-pointer active:scale-95 transition-transform">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[#E29A36] text-[28px] fill-icon" data-icon="refresh">refresh</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="bg-[#FEF6EB] ambient-card rounded-[18px] p-3.5 flex-1 border-l-4 border-[#E29A36] text-left">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-0.5">
<span class="bg-[#FEF6EB] border border-[#E29A36]/40 text-[#E29A36] font-label-sm text-label-sm px-2 py-0.5 rounded-[12px] flex items-center gap-1 font-bold">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#E29A36]"></span>
                  Needs Review
                </span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Spaced Repetition</span>
</div>
<h3 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface text-[15px] leading-tight mt-1">Data Trends &amp; Synonyms</h3>
<p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-[#E29A36] mt-0.5 font-medium">Next milestone: <strong class="text-primary font-semibold">Advanced Mastery</strong></p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Node 4: Current / Active (Right Aligned, Pulsing Lime Glow & Tooltip) -->
<div class="flex items-center flex-row-reverse gap-4 relative text-left">
<div class="relative flex-shrink-0 mr-2 flex items-center justify-center">
<!-- Floating Action Tooltip Above Node -->
<div class="absolute -top-10 right-0 z-20 whitespace-nowrap bg-on-surface text-surface text-label-sm font-label-sm px-3 py-1 rounded-full shadow-lg flex items-center gap-1.5 after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-full after:right-6 after:border-4 after:border-transparent after:border-t-on-surface">
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary-fixed animate-ping"></span>
                Current Lesson
              </div>
<!-- Main Hero Node with Lime Glow -->
<div class="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-surface-container-lowest flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer pulse-glow border-4 border-secondary-fixed relative z-10 shadow-xl active:scale-95 transition-transform">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[36px] fill-icon text-secondary-fixed" data-icon="play_arrow" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">play_arrow</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card Description for Current Node -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest ambient-card rounded-[18px] p-3.5 flex-1 border-2 border-primary-container/20 text-left">
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1">
<span class="bg-secondary-fixed text-on-secondary-fixed font-label-sm text-label-sm px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-[12px] font-bold">Active Drill</span>
</div>
<h3 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold text-[16px] leading-tight">Complex Comparisons</h3>
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2">
<button class="bg-secondary-fixed text-on-secondary-fixed hover:bg-secondary-fixed-dim px-3 py-1.5 rounded-[14px] font-label-md text-label-md font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-xs transition-colors">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]" data-icon="timer">timer</span>
                  Start Lesson • 5 min
                </button>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Node 5: Locked (Left Aligned) -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-4 opacity-60 text-left">
<div class="relative flex-shrink-0 ml-2">
<div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-outline flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-outline-variant shadow-xs">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]" data-icon="lock">lock</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-[18px] p-3 flex-1 border border-outline-variant/40 text-left">
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-outline">Locked</span>
<h3 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface-variant text-[15px] leading-tight">Map &amp; Process Diagrams</h3>
<p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-outline mt-0.5">Unlocks after Lesson 4</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Node 6: Locked Milestone / Trophy (Centered) -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center mt-2 opacity-50">
<div class="w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-outline flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-dashed border-outline shadow-xs mb-2">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[36px]" data-icon="emoji_events">emoji_events</span>
</div>
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest ambient-card rounded-[18px] px-4 py-2.5 max-w-[260px] w-full text-center">
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-outline uppercase tracking-wider">Milestone Gate</span>
<h4 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface-variant text-[15px]">Unit 3 Checkpoint Test</h4>
<p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-outline">Timed Simulation • Full Evaluation</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</main>
<!-- BottomNavBar Component (from Shared Components JSON) -->
<!-- Prohibits border lines per design instructions, using shadow/tonal shift -->
<nav class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full z-50 flex justify-around items-center px-space-xs py-space-sm pb-safe max-w-[390px] mx-auto bg-[#F7F6FC] dark:bg-inverse-surface shadow-md dark:shadow-none">
<!-- Destination 1: Home (Inactive) -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]" data-icon="home">home</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm mt-0.5">Home</span>
</a>
<!-- Destination 2: Learn (Active - Exact JSON mapping logic with Lime indicator dot) -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-primary dark:text-inverse-primary font-bold relative after:content-[''] after:w-1.5 after:h-1.5 after:bg-primary dark:after:bg-secondary-fixed after:rounded-full after:mt-1 active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px] fill-icon text-primary" data-icon="menu_book" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">menu_book</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm mt-0.5">Learn</span>
</a>
<!-- Destination 3: Practice (Inactive) -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]" data-icon="edit_note">edit_note</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm mt-0.5">Practice</span>
</a>
<!-- Destination 4: Progress (Inactive) -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]" data-icon="insights">insights</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm mt-0.5">Progress</span>
</a>
<!-- Destination 5: Profile (Inactive) -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]" data-icon="person">person</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm mt-0.5">Profile</span>
</a>
</nav>
</div>
</html>

<!-- Bandwise — Welcome -->
<!DOCTYPE html><html class="h-full" lang="en"><head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no" name="viewport">
<title>Bandwise - Welcome &amp; Onboarding</title>
<!-- Material Symbols Outlined -->
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<!-- Inter Font -->
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" rel="preconnect">
<link crossorigin="" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" rel="preconnect">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<!-- Tailwind CSS -->
<script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com?plugins=forms,container-queries"></script>
<script id="tailwind-config">
    tailwind.config = {
      darkMode: "class",
      theme: {
        extend: {
          "colors": {
            "on-primary-fixed": "#160066",
            "tertiary-container": "#1b776b",
            "tertiary": "#005d53",
            "outline-variant": "#c8c4d6",
            "on-surface-variant": "#474553",
            "on-tertiary": "#ffffff",
            "on-error": "#ffffff",
            "on-primary-fixed-variant": "#4130ad",
            "on-primary": "#ffffff",
            "on-error-container": "#93000a",
            "surface-container-highest": "#e3dffd",
            "surface-container-lowest": "#ffffff",
            "surface-container-low": "#f6f2ff",
            "surface-bright": "#fcf8ff",
            "on-primary-container": "#eee9ff",
            "primary-container": "#6558d3",
            "error": "#ba1a1a",
            "tertiary-fixed-dim": "#83d5c7",
            "on-background": "#1a192f",
            "surface-container": "#efebff",
            "inverse-primary": "#c6bfff",
            "on-tertiary-fixed": "#00201c",
            "on-secondary-fixed-variant": "#3a4d10",
            "on-secondary-container": "#576b2c",
            "surface": "#fcf8ff",
            "inverse-on-surface": "#f2eeff",
            "on-surface": "#1a192f",
            "surface-container-high": "#e9e5ff",
            "inverse-surface": "#2f2e45",
            "on-tertiary-container": "#a8fbec",
            "secondary-fixed-dim": "#b7d084",
            "secondary-fixed": "#d3ec9e",
            "background": "#fcf8ff",
            "primary-fixed-dim": "#c6bfff",
            "error-container": "#ffdad6",

          },
          "borderRadius": {
            "DEFAULT": "0.25rem",
            "lg": "0.5rem",
            "xl": "0.75rem",
            "2xl": "1.125rem",

            "headline-lg-mobile": ["24px", { "lineHeight": "32px", "letterSpacing": "-0.015em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "headline-sm": ["18px", { "lineHeight": "24px", "letterSpacing": "-0.01em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "label-lg": ["14px", { "lineHeight": "20px", "letterSpacing": "0.01em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "body-md": ["14px", { "lineHeight": "22px", "letterSpacing": "0em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "headline-xl-mobile": ["28px", { "lineHeight": "36px", "letterSpacing": "-0.02em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "headline-md": ["20px", { "lineHeight": "28px", "letterSpacing": "-0.015em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "body-sm": ["13px", { "lineHeight": "18px", "letterSpacing": "0.005em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "headline-xl": ["36px", { "lineHeight": "44px", "letterSpacing": "-0.025em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "label-sm": ["11px", { "lineHeight": "14px", "letterSpacing": "0.04em", "fontWeight": "700" }],

    body {
      min-height: max(884px, 100dvh);

<body class="bg-surface text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen flex justify-center items-center p-0 sm:py-6 selection:bg-secondary-fixed selection:text-on-secondary-fixed" style="background-color: rgb(247, 246, 252);">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Shell (390px Max Width Container) -->
<main class="w-full max-w-[390px] min-h-screen sm:min-h-[844px] bg-surface flex flex-col justify-between px-margin py-space-md sm:rounded-[40px] sm:shadow-2xl sm:border sm:border-outline-variant/30 relative overflow-hidden">
<!-- Ambient Background Accent Glows -->
<div class="absolute -top-12 -left-12 w-48 h-48 bg-primary-fixed/50 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>
<div class="absolute top-1/3 -right-16 w-56 h-56 bg-secondary-fixed/40 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>
<!-- Header Section (Brand Anchor & Utility) -->
<header class="w-full flex items-center justify-between z-10 pt-1">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<!-- Logo Mark -->
<div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm"><div class="flex items-end gap-1 h-5"><span class="w-1.5 h-3 bg-secondary-fixed rounded-full"></span><span class="w-1.5 h-4 bg-primary-fixed rounded-full"></span><span class="w-1.5 h-5 bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-full"></span></div></div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">Bandwise</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant -mt-1 tracking-wider uppercase font-semibold">Academic</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Quick Language / Help Selector -->
<button aria-label="Language and support" class="flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 shadow-sm active:scale-95 transition-transform duration-150" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-sm" data-icon="language">language</span>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface">EN</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-on-surface-variant text-xs" data-icon="expand_more">expand_more</span>
</button>
</header>
<!-- Visual Centerpiece: Geometric IELTS Academic Learning Journey Illustration -->
<section class="w-full my-auto py-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-center relative z-10">
<div class="w-full aspect-[4/3] max-h-[250px] relative flex items-center justify-center">
<!-- Abstract Tactile Vector Canvas -->
<div class="w-full h-full bg-surface-container-low rounded-2xl relative overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center border border-outline-variant/25">
<!-- Curved Stepping Learning Path -->
<svg class="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 358 230" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<path d="M 40 185 C 100 185, 120 120, 180 120 C 240 120, 260 55, 318 55" stroke="#E3DFFD" stroke-dasharray="8 8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="6"></path>
<path d="M 40 185 C 100 185, 120 120, 180 120" stroke="#6558D3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="6"></path>
</svg>
<!-- Module 1: Foundational Practice (Sound Waves / Listening) -->
<div class="absolute left-6 bottom-7 flex flex-col items-center group">
<div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-surface-container-lowest shadow-md border border-outline-variant/30 flex items-center justify-center relative">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container" data-icon="graphic_eq">graphic_eq</span>
<span class="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-secondary-fixed flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-on-secondary-fixed text-[10px]" data-icon="check" data-weight="fill">check</span>
</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-1.5">Listening</span>
</div>
<!-- Module 2: Rigorous Reading & Analysis (Books Geometry) -->
<div class="absolute left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 top-[76px] flex flex-col items-center">
<div class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-primary-container text-on-primary shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2 transform -rotate-1"><div class="flex items-end gap-1 h-4"><span class="w-1 h-2.5 bg-secondary-fixed rounded-full"></span><span class="w-1 h-3.5 bg-white rounded-full"></span><span class="w-1 h-4 bg-secondary-fixed rounded-full"></span></div><div class="flex flex-col text-left"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-primary-container leading-none">Target</span><span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-on-primary leading-tight">Mastery Level</span></div></div>
<!-- Small tactile pulse point -->
<div class="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-primary-container mt-2 ring-4 ring-primary-fixed"></div>
</div>
<!-- Module 3: Speaking & Writing Apex Target Flag -->
<div class="absolute right-6 top-6 flex flex-col items-center"><div class="px-3 py-2 rounded-2xl bg-secondary-fixed border border-secondary-fixed-dim/60 shadow-md flex items-center gap-1.5 text-on-secondary-fixed transform rotate-2"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-lg" data-icon="flag" data-weight="fill">flag</span><span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm font-bold text-on-secondary-fixed">92%</span></div><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold text-secondary mt-1 tracking-tight">Accuracy Goal</span></div>
<!-- Gamified Floating Micro Badges -->
<div class="absolute left-8 top-8 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container-lowest/90 backdrop-blur shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-sm" data-icon="local_fire_department" data-weight="fill">local_fire_department</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-bold">14-Day Sprint</span>
</div>
<div class="absolute right-9 bottom-6 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container-lowest/90 backdrop-blur shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface font-semibold">Rubric-based feedback</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Headline & Pitch Deck Content -->
<div class="w-full text-center mt-5 px-1">
<h1 class="font-headline-xl-mobile text-headline-xl-mobile font-bold text-on-surface tracking-tight leading-tight">
          Master IELTS Academic, <br>
<span class="text-primary-container">one step at a time.</span>
</h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant mt-2.5 max-w-[320px] mx-auto leading-relaxed">
          Bite-sized daily practice tailored to your target band score (6.5 — 8.5+).
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Skill Pillar Micro-tags -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 mt-4">
<span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-label-sm font-label-sm bg-surface-container text-primary font-medium">Reading</span>
<span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-label-sm font-label-sm bg-surface-container text-primary font-medium">Listening</span>
<span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-label-sm font-label-sm bg-surface-container text-primary font-medium">Writing</span>
<span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-label-sm font-label-sm bg-surface-container text-primary font-medium">Speaking</span>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Action Section (Primary & Secondary CTAs with Tactile Affordance) -->
<footer class="w-full flex flex-col gap-2.5 pt-2 pb-1 z-10">
<!-- Primary Action: Sign Up -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] rounded-2xl bg-primary-container text-on-primary font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-md hover:bg-primary transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl" data-icon="mail">mail</span>
<span class="">Sign up with email</span>
</button>
<!-- Secondary Action: Log In -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] rounded-2xl bg-surface-container-lowest text-on-surface font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold border border-outline-variant/70 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]" type="button">
<span class="">Log in</span>
</button>
<!-- Ghost Action: Continue as Guest -->
<div class="w-full text-center pt-1">
<button class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface-variant font-medium py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg hover:text-primary transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95" type="button">
          Continue as guest
        </button>
</div>
<!-- Legal Academic Disclaimer -->
<p class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-outline text-center px-3 pt-1 leading-normal">
        Independent preparation app. Not affiliated with IELTS, British Council, or IDP.
      </p>
</footer>
</main>


</body></html>

<style>
    .material-symbols-outlined {
      font-family: 'Material Symbols Outlined';
      font-weight: normal;
      font-style: normal;
      font-size: 24px;
      line-height: 1;
      letter-spacing: normal;
      text-transform: none;
      display: inline-block;
      white-space: nowrap;
      word-wrap: normal;
      direction: ltr;
      -webkit-font-feature-settings: 'liga';
      -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    }

    @keyframes pulse-subtle {
      0%, 100% { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1); }
      50% { opacity: 0.94; transform: scale(0.985); }
    }

    @keyframes fill-bar {
      0% { width: 12%; }
      50% { width: 68%; }
      100% { width: 94%; }
    }

    @keyframes float-soft {
      0%, 100% { transform: translateY(0); }
      50% { transform: translateY(-4px); }
    }

    .anim-pulse {
      animation: pulse-subtle 4s ease-in-out infinite;
    }
    
    .anim-float {
      animation: float-soft 3.2s ease-in-out infinite;
    }

    .anim-progress {
      animation: fill-bar 3s cubic-bezier(0.65, 0, 0.35, 1) infinite alternate;
    }
  </style>

    body {
      min-height: max(884px, 100dvh);
    }
  </style>

<body class="bg-[#F7F6FC] text-on-surface h-full flex items-center justify-center m-0 p-0 antialiased font-body-md selection:bg-secondary-fixed selection:text-on-secondary-fixed">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Shell (390px simulated container) -->
<main class="w-full max-w-[390px] h-full max-h-[844px] min-h-[884px] bg-[#F7F6FC] flex flex-col justify-between items-center px-6 py-12 relative overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
<!-- Ambient Backdrop Accents (Subtle, non-distracting depth) -->
<div class="absolute -top-24 -right-24 w-64 h-64 bg-surface-container-high/60 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>
<div class="absolute -bottom-24 -left-24 w-72 h-72 bg-secondary-fixed/30 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>
<!-- Top Status / Quiet Area for Native Spacing -->

<!-- Centered Brand Identity Block -->
<section class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center my-auto w-full px-2">
<!-- Logo Symbol: Learning Path & Ascending Steps (Playful geometric tactility) -->
<div class="anim-float relative mb-8 flex items-center justify-center">
<!-- Soft backplate ambient glow -->
<div class="absolute inset-0 bg-primary-container/10 rounded-3xl blur-xl scale-125"></div>
<!-- Geometric Logomark Box -->
<div class="relative w-24 h-24 rounded-3xl bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/30 shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] flex items-center justify-center p-3.5">
<svg class="w-full h-full" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 72 72" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<!-- Step 1 (Foundation): Calm Indigo tint -->
<rect fill="#6558D3" fill-opacity="0.25" height="20" rx="5" width="16" x="8" y="44"></rect>
<!-- Step 2 (Momentum): Core Primary Purple -->
<rect fill="#6558D3" height="36" rx="5" width="16" x="28" y="28"></rect>
<!-- Step 3 (Mastery / Peak): Vibrant Electric Lime Accent -->
<rect fill="#DDF6A7" height="52" rx="5" width="16" x="48" y="12"></rect>
<rect height="52" rx="5" stroke="#25243A" stroke-opacity="0.1" stroke-width="1.5" width="16" x="48" y="12"></rect>
<!-- Ascending Trajectory Spark / Goal Node -->
<circle cx="56" cy="18" fill="#25243A" r="3"></circle>
<path d="M16 40 L36 24 L56 8" stroke="#6558D3" stroke-dasharray="3 3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="2.5"></path>
</svg>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Brand Wordmark -->
<h1 class="font-headline-xl-mobile text-headline-xl-mobile text-[#25243A] font-extrabold tracking-tight mb-2.5">
        Bandwise
      </h1>
<!-- Tagline -->
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant max-w-[260px] mx-auto font-medium">
        Small steps. Stronger English.
      </p>
<!-- Target Mastery Pill -->
<div class="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-full border border-outline-variant/30 shadow-sm">
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary-fixed"></span>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">IELTS Academic Target</span>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Bottom Indicator & Regulatory Quiet Details -->
<footer class="w-full flex flex-col items-center space-y-7 pb-2">
<!-- Minimal Progress Indicator Deck -->
<div class="w-full max-w-[190px] flex flex-col items-center gap-2.5">
<div class="w-full h-1.5 bg-surface-container-high rounded-full overflow-hidden p-0.5 relative">
<div class="anim-progress h-full bg-[#DDF6A7] rounded-full shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(221,246,167,0.8)] border border-[#25243A]/10"></div>
</div>
<!-- Subtle Dot Rhythm Indicator -->
<div class="flex items-center space-x-2 opacity-60">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></span>
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-outline-variant"></span>
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-outline-variant"></span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Discrete Clarification / Academic Prep Disclaimer -->
<div class="text-center">
<p class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-outline tracking-normal font-medium">
          Independent IELTS Academic prep
        </p>
</div>
</footer>
</main>


</body></html>

<!-- Bandwise — Home -->
<!DOCTYPE html><html class="light" lang="en"><head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" name="viewport">
<title>Bandwise - Home Dashboard</title>
<!-- Material Symbols Outlined -->
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<!-- Inter Google Font -->
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" rel="preconnect">
<link crossorigin="" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" rel="preconnect">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<!-- Tailwind CSS -->

<style>
    .material-symbols-outlined {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 0, 'wght' 500, 'GRAD' 0, 'opsz' 24;

      line-height: 1;
    }

      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;
    }

      background-color: #f7f6fc;
      -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
      -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;

      min-height: max(884px, 100dvh);

<body class="text-on-surface antialiased flex justify-center min-h-screen">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Shell (390px max canvas) -->
<div class="w-full max-w-[390px] min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F7F6FC] relative pb-28">
<!-- Top App Bar (Shared Components Anchor) -->
<header class="sticky top-0 left-0 w-full z-40 flex justify-between items-center px-margin h-16 max-w-screen-md mx-auto bg-surface dark:bg-inverse-surface shadow-sm">
<div class="flex items-center space-x-2">
<div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed flex items-center justify-center text-primary font-bold text-label-md overflow-hidden ring-2 ring-primary/20">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-primary">AM</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm font-bold text-primary dark:text-inverse-primary tracking-tight">Bandwise</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center space-x-2">

</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Scrollable Dashboard Content -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-3 space-y-4">
<!-- Greeting & Target Row -->
<section class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div>
<h1 class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5">
            Hi, Alex <span class="text-xl">👋</span>
</h1>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Ready for your daily sprint?</p>
</div>

</section>
<!-- Stats Header Row: Micro Cards (Streak, XP, Goal Ring) -->
<section class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
<!-- Streak Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-2xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm border border-on-surface/[0.04]">
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 text-on-secondary-fixed-variant">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill text-base text-warning" data-icon="local_fire_department" style="font-variation-settings: &quot;FILL&quot; 1;">local_fire_department</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Streak</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-2">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">14</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant ml-0.5">days</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="inline-block w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-secondary-fixed-dim"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-secondary font-semibold">Active</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Daily XP Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-2xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-sm border border-on-surface/[0.04]">
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 text-on-secondary-fixed-variant">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill text-base text-secondary" data-icon="bolt" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">bolt</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Total XP</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-2">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">320</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant ml-0.5">Total XP</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-semibold">+45 today</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Daily Goal Progress Ring Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-2xl p-3 flex flex-col items-center justify-between shadow-sm border border-on-surface/[0.04] relative">
<div class="w-full flex justify-between items-center text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Daily Goal</span>
</div>
<!-- Circular Ring -->
<div class="relative w-12 h-12 my-1 flex items-center justify-center">
<svg class="w-full h-full transform -rotate-90" viewBox="0 0 36 36">
<!-- Background circle -->
<path class="text-surface-container-high" d="M18 2.0845 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 31.831 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 -31.831" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3.5"></path>
<!-- Accent progress circle (75% completed) -->
<path class="text-secondary-fixed stroke-current" d="M18 2.0845 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 31.831 a 15.9155 15.9155 0 0 1 0 -31.831" fill="none" stroke-dasharray="75, 100" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-width="3.8"></path>
</svg>
<div class="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] font-bold text-on-surface leading-none">15</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[8px] text-on-surface-variant leading-none mt-0.5">/20m</span>
</div>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-secondary font-semibold">5m left</span>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Hero "Continue Learning" Card -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-on-surface/[0.05] relative overflow-hidden">
<!-- Soft Tint Graphic Decorator -->
<div class="absolute -right-6 -bottom-6 w-28 h-28 bg-surface-container rounded-full opacity-60 pointer-events-none"></div>
<div class="absolute right-4 top-4">
<span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-[12px] bg-secondary-fixed text-on-secondary-fixed">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg" data-icon="menu_book">menu_book</span>
</span>
</div>
<div class="relative z-10">
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed mb-2">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary animate-pulse"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-semibold">Unit 4 • Academic Reading</span>
</div>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold tracking-tight mb-2">
            Skimming &amp; Scanning Complex Texts
          </h2>
<div class="flex items-center justify-between text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm mb-1.5">
<span class="">Overall Progress</span>
<span class="font-semibold text-primary">65% complete</span>
</div>
<!-- Progress Bar with Lime Accent -->
<div class="w-full bg-surface-container h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden mb-4">
<div class="bg-secondary-fixed h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style="width: 65%;"></div>
</div>
<!-- Action Button -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
<div class="flex items-center text-on-surface-variant font-body-sm text-body-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-base mr-1 text-primary" data-icon="schedule">schedule</span>
<span class="">Est. 8 mins</span>
</div>
<button class="bg-primary-container text-on-primary font-label-lg text-label-lg px-5 py-2.5 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm hover:opacity-95 active:scale-95 transition-all duration-150 flex items-center gap-1.5">
<span class="">Resume Lesson</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-base" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
</section><section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-3.5 shadow-sm border border-on-surface/[0.05] flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] transition-all"><div class="flex items-center gap-3"><div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-[12px] bg-warning-tint text-warning flex items-center justify-center"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-xl" data-icon="style">style</span></div><div><div class="flex items-center gap-1.5"><span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-warning font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Up next for review</span></div><p class="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface leading-tight">12 vocabulary cards due</p></div></div><button class="bg-surface-container text-primary hover:bg-primary hover:text-on-primary font-label-sm text-label-sm px-3 py-1.5 rounded-[14px] transition-colors flex items-center gap-1"><span class="">Review</span><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-sm" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span></button></section>
<!-- Section: Skill Shortcuts (2x2 Grid) -->
<section class="space-y-2.5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between px-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">Skill Shortcuts</h3>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-primary font-medium hover:underline cursor-pointer">View all</span>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5"><!-- 1. Listening -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest p-3.5 rounded-[18px] border border-on-surface/[0.04] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-[12px] bg-skill-listening-tint flex items-center justify-center text-skill-listening">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg" data-icon="headphones">headphones</span>
</div>
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-skill-listening-tint text-skill-listening font-label-sm text-label-sm">88% Accuracy</span>
</div>
<div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-skill-listening uppercase tracking-wider block font-bold">Listening</span>
<h4 class="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface mt-0.5 leading-snug">Section 3 Prep</h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1">Multi-speaker dialogs</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 2. Reading -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest p-3.5 rounded-[18px] border border-on-surface/[0.04] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-[12px] bg-skill-reading-tint flex items-center justify-center text-skill-reading">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg" data-icon="menu_book">menu_book</span>
</div>
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-skill-reading-tint text-skill-reading font-label-sm text-label-sm">High Yield</span>
</div>
<div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-skill-reading uppercase tracking-wider block font-bold">Reading</span>
<h4 class="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface mt-0.5 leading-snug">True / False / Not Given</h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1">Rapid verification drills</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 3. Writing -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest p-3.5 rounded-[18px] border border-on-surface/[0.04] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-[12px] bg-skill-writing-tint flex items-center justify-center text-skill-writing">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg" data-icon="edit_note">edit_note</span>
</div>
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-skill-writing-tint text-skill-writing font-label-sm text-label-sm">92% Accuracy</span>
</div>
<div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-skill-writing uppercase tracking-wider block font-bold">Writing</span>
<h4 class="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface mt-0.5 leading-snug">Task 1 Chart Analysis</h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1">Comparative trends</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 4. Speaking -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest p-3.5 rounded-[18px] border border-on-surface/[0.04] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-[12px] bg-skill-speaking-tint flex items-center justify-center text-skill-speaking">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg" data-icon="mic">mic</span>
</div>
<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-skill-speaking-tint text-skill-speaking font-label-sm text-label-sm">AI Coach</span>
</div>
<div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-skill-speaking uppercase tracking-wider block font-bold">Speaking</span>
<h4 class="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface mt-0.5 leading-snug">Part 2 Cue Card drills</h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1">Fluency &amp; coherence</p>
</div>
</div></div>
</section>
<!-- Section: Weekly Activity Bar Chart Card -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-on-surface/[0.05] space-y-3">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div>
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">Weekly Activity</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Consistent daily practice</p>
</div>
<div class="text-right">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-primary font-bold">4.1 hrs</span>
<span class="block font-label-sm text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">this week</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 7-Day Bar Chart: Mon to Sun -->
<div class="pt-3 pb-1">
<div class="h-28 flex items-end justify-between gap-2 px-1">
<!-- Mon: 35m -->
<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 h-full justify-end">
<div class="w-full bg-primary-container/20 rounded-t-lg transition-all" style="height: 40%;"></div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">M</span>
</div>
<!-- Tue: 50m -->
<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 h-full justify-end">
<div class="w-full bg-primary-container/30 rounded-t-lg transition-all" style="height: 60%;"></div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">T</span>
</div>
<!-- Wed: 45m -->
<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 h-full justify-end">
<div class="w-full bg-primary-container/25 rounded-t-lg transition-all" style="height: 52%;"></div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">W</span>
</div>
<!-- Thu: 60m -->
<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 h-full justify-end">
<div class="w-full bg-primary-container/40 rounded-t-lg transition-all" style="height: 72%;"></div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">T</span>
</div>
<!-- Fri: Today (Highlighted Lime + Purple) -->
<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 h-full justify-end">
<div class="w-full bg-gradient-to-t from-primary-container to-secondary-fixed rounded-t-lg ring-2 ring-secondary-fixed/50 transition-all shadow-sm relative group" style="height: 85%;">
<span class="absolute -top-5 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-on-surface text-surface text-[9px] font-bold px-1 rounded">55m</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-primary font-bold">F</span>
</div>
<!-- Sat: Target 30m -->
<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 h-full justify-end"><div class="w-full border border-dashed border-outline-variant rounded-t-lg transition-all" style="height: 30%;"></div><span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">S</span></div>
<!-- Sun: Planned -->
<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 h-full justify-end"><div class="w-full border border-dashed border-outline-variant rounded-t-lg transition-all" style="height: 20%;"></div><span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant">S</span></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Weekly Summary Footer Note -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-[12px] px-3 py-2 flex items-center justify-between text-on-surface-variant">
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-secondary text-sm" data-icon="check_circle">check_circle</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Daily quota maintained for 5 days</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary font-semibold">+120 XP bonus</span>
</div>
</section>
</main>
<!-- Bottom Navigation Bar (Shared Components Anchor: Destination Count 5) -->
<nav class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full z-50 flex justify-around items-center px-space-xs py-space-sm pb-safe max-w-screen-md mx-auto bg-surface dark:bg-inverse-surface shadow-md">
<!-- 1. Home (ACTIVE) -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-primary dark:text-inverse-primary font-bold relative after:content-[''] after:w-1.5 after:h-1.5 after:bg-primary dark:after:bg-secondary-fixed after:rounded-full after:mt-1 hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl fill" data-icon="home">home</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm">Home</span>
</a>
<!-- 2. Learn -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl" data-icon="menu_book">menu_book</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm">Learn</span>
</a>
<!-- 3. Practice -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl" data-icon="edit_note">edit_note</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm">Practice</span>
</a>
<!-- 4. Progress -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl" data-icon="insights">insights</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm">Progress</span>
</a>
<!-- 5. Profile -->
<a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant dark:text-outline-variant hover:text-primary dark:hover:text-inverse-primary transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-2xl" data-icon="person">person</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm">Profile</span>
</a>
</nav>
</div>




</body></html>

      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 0, 'wght' 500, 'GRAD' 0, 'opsz' 24;

<body class="h-full flex flex-col justify-between antialiased selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">
<!-- Container for Mobile Onboarding Screen -->
<div class="w-full max-w-lg mx-auto flex flex-col min-h-screen bg-[#F7F6FC]">
<!-- Shared Component: TopAppBar (Configured for Onboarding Step 2 of 6) -->
<header class="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-md px-4 h-16 w-full flex items-center justify-between shadow-sm">
<!-- Leading Back Action -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="p-2 -ml-2 rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95 text-on-surface" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined block text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Centered Progress Cluster -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 px-3">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-semibold tracking-wider uppercase">Step 2 of 6</span>
<!-- 6-Segment Progress Bar -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-1 w-32 max-w-full">
<!-- Segment 1: Filled -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 2: Filled (Current) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 3: Inactive -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<!-- Segment 4: Inactive -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<!-- Segment 5: Inactive -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<!-- Segment 6: Inactive -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Trailing Action -->
<button class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface-variant hover:text-primary transition-colors p-2 -mr-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-surface-container-high active:scale-95 duration-150" type="button">
        Skip
      </button>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-4 pb-6 flex flex-col">
<!-- Header Group -->
<div class="mb-5">
<h1 class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface mb-1.5">
          When is your IELTS exam?
        </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          We'll build a tailored weekly pace leading up to test day.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Inline Calendar Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] mb-4">
<!-- Month Navigation Header -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">
            November 2025
          </h2>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1">
<button aria-label="Previous month" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container hover:text-on-surface active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] block">chevron_left</span>
</button>
<button aria-label="Next month" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container hover:text-on-surface active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] block">chevron_right</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Days of Week Row (M T W T F S S) -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-7 mb-2 text-center">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/70 font-semibold">M</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/70 font-semibold">T</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/70 font-semibold">W</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/70 font-semibold">T</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/70 font-semibold">F</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/70 font-semibold">S</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/70 font-semibold">S</span>
</div>
<!-- Calendar Days Grid -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-y-1 text-center items-center">
<!-- Leading empty/previous month days (Oct 27-31) -->
<span class="py-2 text-outline-variant font-body-sm text-body-sm select-none">27</span>
<span class="py-2 text-outline-variant font-body-sm text-body-sm select-none">28</span>
<span class="py-2 text-outline-variant font-body-sm text-body-sm select-none">29</span>
<span class="py-2 text-outline-variant font-body-sm text-body-sm select-none">30</span>
<span class="py-2 text-outline-variant font-body-sm text-body-sm select-none">31</span>
<!-- November 1 - 2 -->
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">1</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">2</button>
<!-- Nov 3 - 9 (Nov 3 is today, circled) -->
<button class="relative py-2 flex items-center justify-center font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-primary rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border border-primary-container flex items-center justify-center">3</span>
</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">4</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">5</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">6</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">7</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">8</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">9</button>
<!-- Nov 10 - 16 -->
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">10</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">11</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">12</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">13</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">14</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">15</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">16</button>
<!-- Nov 17 - 23 (Nov 22 is SELECTED) -->
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">17</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">18</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">19</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">20</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">21</button>
<!-- Selected Day (22) -->
<div class="py-1 flex items-center justify-center">
<button class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-white font-semibold font-body-md text-body-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm ring-2 ring-primary-container/20 active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
              22
            </button>
</div>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">23</button>
<!-- Nov 24 - 30 -->
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">24</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">25</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">26</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">27</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">28</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">29</button>
<button class="py-2 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">30</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Preparation Indicator Banner (Lime Accent Tint) -->
<div class="bg-[#DDF6A7]/30 border border-[#DDF6A7] rounded-[14px] p-3 flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#DDF6A7] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-on-surface text-[15px]">
          ⚡
        </div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface font-bold tracking-tight">
            8 weeks to prepare
          </span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium">
            Recommended pace: 4 lessons / week
          </span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Toggle Row Card ("I haven't booked my exam yet") -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex justify-between items-center mt-3">
<label class="font-body-md text-body-md font-medium text-on-surface cursor-pointer select-none pr-3" for="exam-toggle">
          I haven't booked my exam yet
        </label>
<!-- Custom Subtle Switch (Unselected/Off state) -->
<button aria-checked="false" class="relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-full border-2 border-transparent bg-surface-dim transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container focus:ring-offset-2" id="exam-toggle" role="switch" type="button">
<span class="sr-only">Toggle booked exam</span>
<span aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none inline-block h-5 w-5 transform rounded-full bg-white shadow ring-0 transition duration-200 ease-in-out translate-x-0"></span>
</button>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom CTA Section -->
<div class="sticky bottom-0 z-20 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md px-4 py-4 border-t border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]">
<button class="w-full bg-primary-container text-white font-semibold py-4 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm text-center font-label-lg text-label-lg hover:brightness-95 active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
</body></html>

            "headline-md": ["20px", { "lineHeight": "28px", "letterSpacing": "-0.015em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "headline-xl-mobile": ["28px", { "lineHeight": "36px", "letterSpacing": "-0.02em", "fontWeight": "700" }]
          }
        }
      }
    };
  </script>

<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Reminder Card Section -->

<div>

<!-- Toggle Switch ON -->

<div class="w-12 h-7 bg-surface-dim peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer peer-checked:after:translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:left-[2px] after:bg-surface-container-lowest after:border-outline-variant after:border after:rounded-full after:h-6 after:w-6 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-primary-container"></div>

</div>
<button class="text-label-md font-label-md text-primary font-bold hover:bg-surface-container-high px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full transition-colors active:scale-95">

</div>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom CTA Container -->
<footer class="sticky bottom-0 w-full bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur-md px-4 py-4 max-w-lg mx-auto">

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</footer>
</div>

<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-semibold">Not sure yet</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">We'll suggest one after your initial check</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Unchecked Radio Indicator -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center"></div>
</label>
</section>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom Container -->
<footer class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md">
<div class="max-w-lg mx-auto px-4 pt-3 pb-6 sm:pb-8">
<button class="w-full bg-primary-container text-white font-headline-sm text-headline-sm font-semibold py-4 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm text-center active:scale-[0.99] active:brightness-95 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</footer>
</div>
<script>
    // Lightweight radio selection state handler
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.selection-card');
    cards.forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', () => {
        cards.forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'bg-[#F4EFFF]');
          c.classList.add('border-[1.5px]', 'border-on-surface/10', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
          const radio = c.querySelector('div:last-child');
          if (radio) {
            radio.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center';
            radio.innerHTML = '';
          }
        });

        card.classList.remove('border-[1.5px]', 'border-on-surface/10', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
        card.classList.add('border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'bg-[#F4EFFF]');
        const activeRadio = card.querySelector('div:last-child');
        if (activeRadio) {
          activeRadio.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center text-secondary-container shadow-sm';
          activeRadio.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] font-bold" data-icon="check" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">check</span>';
        }
      });
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

<body class="min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between items-center selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Wrapper -->
<div class="w-full max-w-lg min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F7F6FC] relative">
<!-- Top Navigation Bar (Shared Component Execution) -->
<header class="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-[#F7F6FC]/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<div class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-4 h-16 max-w-lg mx-auto">
<!-- Leading Action: Back Arrow Icon -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="p-2 -ml-2 rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95 transition-transform" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-on-surface" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Centered Header & Segmented Progress Indicator -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 px-4">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold mb-1.5 tracking-tight">Step 3 of 6</span>
<!-- 6-Segment Progress Bar -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-32 max-w-[140px]">
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Trailing Action: Skip -->
<button class="font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold text-on-surface-variant hover:text-primary hover:bg-surface-container-high px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95 transition-transform" type="button">
          Skip
        </button>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-3 pb-8 flex flex-col">
<!-- Title & Micro-Prompt -->
<section class="mb-5">
<h1 class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight mb-2">
          What feels closest to your current English level?
        </h1>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-outline text-[18px]" data-icon="info">info</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline">
            A self-assessment, not an official CEFR level.
          </p>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Level Selection List -->
<div aria-label="Current English Level" class="space-y-3" role="radiogroup">
<!-- Card 1: Beginner -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Beginner</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can understand basic greetings and isolated words
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 2: Elementary -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Elementary</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can understand simple everyday sentences and questions
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 3: Intermediate (SELECTED STATE) -->
<div aria-checked="true" class="relative bg-[#F4EFFF] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border-2 border-primary-container cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ring-4 ring-primary-container/10" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">Intermediate</h3>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant leading-snug font-normal">
              I can follow short everyday conversations and express familiar ideas
            </p>
</div>
<!-- Selected Checkmark Badge -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-white text-[16px] font-bold" data-icon="check">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 4: Upper-Intermediate -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Upper-Intermediate</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can read standard articles and communicate comfortably in discussions
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 5: Advanced -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Advanced</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can express complex arguments fluently and understand nuanced texts
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom Container -->
<div class="sticky bottom-0 z-30 w-full bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md px-4 pt-3 pb-6 border-t border-transparent shadow-[0_-8px_20px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]">
<button class="w-full bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary text-on-primary font-headline-sm text-headline-sm font-semibold py-4 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-150" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<script>
    // Lightweight interaction logic for radio selection states
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      const cards = document.querySelectorAll('[role="radio"]');
      
      cards.forEach(card => {
        card.addEventListener('click', () => {
          // Deselect all
          cards.forEach(c => {
            c.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
            c.className = "group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3";
            const indicator = c.querySelector('.shrink-0');
            indicator.className = "w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary";
            indicator.innerHTML = '<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>';
            const textDesc = c.querySelector('p');
            textDesc.className = "font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug";
          });

          // Select current
          card.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
          card.className = "relative bg-[#F4EFFF] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border-2 border-primary-container cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ring-4 ring-primary-container/10";
          const indicator = card.querySelector('.shrink-0');
          indicator.className = "w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm";
          indicator.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-white text-[16px] font-bold" data-icon="check">check</span>';
          const textDesc = card.querySelector('p');
          textDesc.className = "font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant leading-snug font-normal";
        });
      });
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

    .material-symbols-outlined.fill-icon {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1, 'wght' 600, 'GRAD' 0, 'opsz' 24;
    }
  </style>

      min-height: max(884px, 100dvh);

<body class="min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between items-center selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-primary">
