<!-- Leading Icon -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Centered Progress Group -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 flex-1 max-w-[190px] px-2">
<span class="text-label-md font-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">Step 1 of 6</span>
<!-- 6-Segment Progress Bar -->
<div aria-valuemax="6" aria-valuemin="1" aria-valuenow="5" class="flex items-center gap-1 w-full" role="progressbar">
<!-- Segment 1 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 2 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 3 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 4 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 5 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 6 (Empty/Subtle) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Trailing Action -->
<button class="h-10 px-3 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-label-lg font-label-lg font-semibold text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container-high hover:text-on-surface transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" type="button">
          Skip
        </button>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Area -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-3 pb-8 flex flex-col justify-start">
<!-- Hero Header -->
<div class="mb-6 space-y-1.5 text-left">
<h1 class="text-headline-lg-mobile font-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight">
          Which skills need the most focus?
        </h1>
<p class="text-body-md font-body-md text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed">
          Pick one or more to personalize your daily sprint.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- 2x2 Bento Selection Grid -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3.5 w-full">
<!-- 1. Listening (Unselected) -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-on-surface/[0.08] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 hover:shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">
<!-- Icon Badge Tinted -->

</div>
<!-- Unselected Circle Indicator -->

</div>
<div class="mt-3">

</div>
</div>
<!-- 2. Reading (Unselected) -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-on-surface/[0.08] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 hover:shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">

</div>

</div>

</div>
</div>
<!-- 3. Writing (SELECTED) -->
<div aria-checked="true" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-[#F5F3FF] border-2 border-primary-container shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">
<!-- Icon Badge Tinted -->

</div>
<!-- Selected Checkmark Circle -->

<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill-icon text-white text-[15px]" data-icon="check" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check</span>
</div>
</div>

<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-0.5">

</div>
<p class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal leading-snug">Task 1 charts &amp; Task 2 essays</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 4. Speaking (SELECTED) -->
<div aria-checked="true" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-[#F5F3FF] border-2 border-primary-container shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">

<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]" data-icon="mic">mic</span>
</div>
<!-- Selected Checkmark Circle -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs">

</div>
</div>

<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-0.5">

</div>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Informative Subtext Banner -->
<div class="mt-6 p-3.5 bg-surface-container-lowest/80 rounded-[14px] border border-outline-variant/30 flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-secondary-container/40 flex items-center justify-center text-on-secondary-container flex-shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]" data-icon="tune">tune</span>
</div>
<p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
          You can adapt these priorities or add listening drills anytime in your study plan.
        </p>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom CTA Section -->
<footer class="sticky bottom-0 w-full p-4 pt-3 pb-6 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md">
<div class="max-w-lg mx-auto">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container hover:bg-surface-tint active:scale-[0.99] text-white text-body-lg font-semibold rounded-[14px] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(101,88,211,0.25)] flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-150" type="button">
          Continue
        </button>
</div>
</footer>
</div>
<!-- Interactive State Toggle Script -->
<script>
    document.querySelectorAll('[role="checkbox"]').forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isChecked = card.getAttribute('aria-checked') === 'true';
        const indicator = card.querySelector('.rounded-full:last-child');
        
        if (isChecked) {
          card.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
          card.classList.remove('bg-[#F5F3FF]', 'border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)]');
          card.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'border', 'border-on-surface/[0.08]', 'shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]');
          
          indicator.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center transition-colors';
          indicator.innerHTML = '';
        } else {
          card.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
          card.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'border', 'border-on-surface/[0.08]', 'shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]');
          card.classList.add('bg-[#F5F3FF]', 'border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)]');
          
          indicator.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs';
          indicator.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill-icon text-white text-[15px]" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">check</span>';
        }
      });
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

</button>

<!-- Segment 4: Gray -->

<!-- Segment 5: Gray -->

6.5

</div>
<!-- Unchecked Radio Indicator -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center"></div>
</label>
<!-- 6. Not sure yet -->
<label class="selection-card relative flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] border-[1.5px] border-on-surface/10 shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:border-primary-container/40">
<input class="sr-only" name="target_band" type="radio" value="unsure"/>
<div class="flex items-center gap-3.5">
<div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-surface-container flex items-center justify-center text-primary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]" data-icon="help_outline">help_outline</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-semibold">Not sure yet</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">We'll suggest one after your initial check</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Unchecked Radio Indicator -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center"></div>
</label></section>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom Container -->
<footer class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md">
<div class="max-w-lg mx-auto px-4 pt-3 pb-6 sm:pb-8">
<button class="w-full bg-primary-container text-white font-headline-sm text-headline-sm font-semibold py-4 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm text-center active:scale-[0.99] active:brightness-95 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</footer>
</div>
<script>
    // Lightweight radio selection state handler
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.selection-card');
    cards.forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', () => {
        cards.forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'bg-[#F4EFFF]');
          c.classList.add('border-[1.5px]', 'border-on-surface/10', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
          const radio = c.querySelector('div:last-child');
          if (radio) {
            radio.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center';
            radio.innerHTML = '';
          }
        });

        card.classList.remove('border-[1.5px]', 'border-on-surface/10', 'bg-surface-container-lowest');
        card.classList.add('border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'bg-[#F4EFFF]');
        const activeRadio = card.querySelector('div:last-child');
        if (activeRadio) {
          activeRadio.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center text-secondary-container shadow-sm';
          activeRadio.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] font-bold" data-icon="check" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">check</span>';
        }
      });
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

<body class="h-full flex flex-col justify-between antialiased selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">
<!-- Container for Mobile Onboarding Screen -->

<header class="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-md px-4 h-16 w-full flex items-center justify-between">
<!-- Leading Back Action -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="p-2 -ml-2 rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95 text-on-surface" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined block text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Centered Progress Cluster -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 px-3"><span class="text-[14px] font-medium text-[#25243A]">Step 2 of 6</span><div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-36 max-w-full"><div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div><div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div><div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#E3DFFD]"></div><div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#E3DFFD]"></div><div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#E3DFFD]"></div><div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#E3DFFD]"></div></div></div>
<!-- Trailing Action -->
<button class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface-variant hover:text-primary transition-colors p-2 -mr-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-surface-container-high active:scale-95 duration-150" type="button">
        Skip
      </button>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-4 pb-6 flex flex-col"><!-- Header Group -->
<div class="mb-5">
<h1 class="text-[24px] font-bold text-[#1A192F] tracking-tight mb-1.5">
    When is your IELTS exam?
  </h1>
<p class="text-[14px] text-[#474553]">
    We'll build a tailored weekly pace leading up to test day.
  </p>
</div>
<!-- Inline Calendar Card -->
<div class="bg-white rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border border-[#25243A]/5 mb-4 transition-all duration-200" id="calendar-card">
<!-- Month Navigation Header -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-2">
<h2 class="text-[18px] font-bold text-[#1A192F]">
        May 2025
      </h2>
<span class="text-[12px] font-medium text-[#6558D3] bg-[#E3DFFD]/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">Current Month</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1">
<button aria-label="Previous month" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-[#787585] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] hover:text-[#1A192F] active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] block">chevron_left</span>
</button>
<button aria-label="Next month" class="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-[#787585] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] hover:text-[#1A192F] active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] block">chevron_right</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Days of Week Row -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-7 mb-2 text-center">
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#787585] font-semibold">M</span>
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#787585] font-semibold">T</span>
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#787585] font-semibold">W</span>
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#787585] font-semibold">T</span>
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#787585] font-semibold">F</span>
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#787585] font-semibold">S</span>
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#787585] font-semibold">S</span>
</div>
<!-- Calendar Days Grid for May 2025 (May 1st is Thursday) -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-y-1 text-center items-center font-medium text-[14px]">
<!-- Apr 28-30 (Previous month days, disabled) -->
<span class="py-2 text-[#C8C4D6] opacity-40 select-none">28</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#C8C4D6] opacity-40 select-none">29</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#C8C4D6] opacity-40 select-none">30</span>
<!-- Past Days: May 1-4 (Disabled with opacity-30) -->
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">1</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">2</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">3</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">4</span>
<!-- May 5-11 (Disabled past days) -->
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">5</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">6</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">7</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">8</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">9</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">10</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">11</span>
<!-- May 12-18 (Disabled past days) -->
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">12</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">13</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">14</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">15</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">16</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">17</span>
<span class="py-2 text-[#787585] opacity-30 pointer-events-none select-none">18</span>
<!-- Today: May 19 (Circled/highlighted) -->
<div class="py-1 flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-[#6558D3] text-[#4C3DB9] font-bold flex items-center justify-center select-none shadow-sm" title="Today">
        19
      </span>
</div>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">20</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">21</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">22</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">23</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">24</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">25</button>
<!-- May 26-31 -->
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">26</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">27</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">28</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">29</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">30</button>
<button class="py-2 text-[#1A192F] hover:bg-[#EFEBFF] rounded-full active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">31</button>
<span class="py-2 text-[#C8C4D6] opacity-40 select-none">1</span>
</div>
<!-- Selected exam date notification / quick jump -->
<div class="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-[#25243A]/5 flex items-center justify-between text-[13px]">
<span class="text-[#474553]">Selected Exam Date:</span>
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#6558D3] text-white font-semibold px-3 py-1 rounded-full shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[15px]">event</span>
<span>July 14, 2025</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Preparation Indicator Banner -->
<div class="bg-[#F0FAD2] border border-[#D3EC9E] rounded-[18px] p-3.5 flex items-center gap-3 transition-all duration-200" id="prep-banner">
<div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-[#D3EC9E] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 text-[#3A4D10]">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">bolt</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="text-[15px] text-[#141F00] font-bold tracking-tight">
      8 weeks to prepare
    </span>
<span class="text-[12px] text-[#3A4D10] font-medium">
      Ideal duration for a +1.0 Band Score improvement
    </span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Toggle Row Card ("I haven't booked my exam yet") -->
<div class="bg-white rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border border-[#25243A]/5 flex justify-between items-center mt-3 cursor-pointer select-none" onclick="(function(){
  var btn = document.getElementById('exam-toggle');
  var thumb = document.getElementById('toggle-thumb');
  var cal = document.getElementById('calendar-card');
  var banner = document.getElementById('prep-banner');
  var isChecked = btn.getAttribute('aria-checked') === 'true';
  var next = !isChecked;
  btn.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(next));
  if (next) {
    btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-dim', 'bg-[#dad7f4]');
    btn.classList.add('bg-[#6558D3]');
    thumb.classList.remove('translate-x-0');
    thumb.classList.add('translate-x-5');
    cal.classList.add('opacity-40', 'pointer-events-none');
    banner.classList.add('hidden');
  } else {
    btn.classList.add('bg-surface-dim');
    btn.classList.remove('bg-[#6558D3]');
    thumb.classList.add('translate-x-0');
    thumb.classList.remove('translate-x-5');
    cal.classList.remove('opacity-40', 'pointer-events-none');
    banner.classList.remove('hidden');
  }
})()">
<label class="text-[14px] font-medium text-[#1A192F] cursor-pointer pr-3" for="exam-toggle">
    I haven't booked my exam yet
  </label>
<!-- Custom Toggle Switch -->
<button aria-checked="false" class="relative inline-flex h-6 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded-full border-2 border-transparent bg-surface-dim transition-colors duration-200 ease-in-out focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container focus:ring-offset-2" id="exam-toggle" role="switch" type="button">
<span class="sr-only">Toggle booked exam</span>
<span aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none inline-block h-5 w-5 transform rounded-full bg-white shadow ring-0 transition duration-200 ease-in-out translate-x-0" id="toggle-thumb"></span>
</button>
</div></main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom CTA Section -->
<div class="sticky bottom-0 z-20 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md px-4 py-4 border-t border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]">
<button class="w-full bg-primary-container text-white font-semibold py-4 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm text-center font-label-lg text-label-lg hover:brightness-95 active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
</body></html>

<!-- Bandwise — Onboarding: Current Level -->

            "headline-xl-mobile": ["28px", { "lineHeight": "36px", "letterSpacing": "-0.02em", "fontWeight": "700" }]

<body class="min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between items-center selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Wrapper -->
<div class="w-full max-w-lg min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F7F6FC] relative">
<!-- Top Navigation Bar (Shared Component Execution) -->
<header class="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-[#F7F6FC]/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<div class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-4 h-16 max-w-lg mx-auto">
<!-- Leading Action: Back Arrow Icon -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="p-2 -ml-2 rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95 transition-transform" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-on-surface" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Centered Header & Segmented Progress Indicator -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 px-4">
<span class="text-sm font-medium text-[#25243A] mb-1.5 tracking-tight">Step 3 of 6</span>
<!-- 6-Segment Progress Bar -->
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-36">
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Trailing Action: Skip -->
<button class="font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold text-on-surface-variant hover:text-primary hover:bg-surface-container-high px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95 transition-transform" type="button">
          Skip
        </button>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-3 pb-8 flex flex-col">
<!-- Title & Micro-Prompt -->
<section class="mb-5">
<h1 class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight mb-2">
          What feels closest to your current English level?
        </h1>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-outline text-[18px]" data-icon="info">info</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline">
            A self-assessment, not an official CEFR level.
          </p>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Level Selection List -->
<div aria-label="Current English Level" class="space-y-3" role="radiogroup">
<!-- Card 1: Beginner -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Beginner</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can understand basic greetings and isolated words
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 2: Elementary -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Elementary</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can understand simple everyday sentences and questions
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 3: Intermediate (SELECTED STATE) -->
<div aria-checked="true" class="relative bg-[#F5F3FF] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border-2 border-[#6558D3] cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold">Intermediate</h3>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant leading-snug font-normal">
              I can follow short everyday conversations and express familiar ideas
            </p>
</div>
<!-- Selected Checkmark Badge -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-white text-[16px] font-bold" data-icon="check">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 4: Upper-Intermediate -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Upper-Intermediate</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can read standard articles and communicate comfortably in discussions
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Card 5: Advanced -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3" role="radio" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex-1 pr-1">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-bold mb-0.5">Advanced</h3>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug">
              I can express complex arguments fluently and understand nuanced texts
            </p>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary">
<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom Container -->
<div class="sticky bottom-0 z-30 w-full bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md px-4 pt-3 pb-6 border-t border-transparent shadow-[0_-8px_20px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]">
<button class="w-full bg-[#6558D3] hover:bg-primary text-on-primary font-headline-sm text-headline-sm font-semibold py-4 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-150" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<script>
    // Lightweight interaction logic for radio selection states
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      const cards = document.querySelectorAll('[role="radio"]');
      
      cards.forEach(card => {
        card.addEventListener('click', () => {
          // Deselect all
          cards.forEach(c => {
            c.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
            c.className = "group relative bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 hover:border-primary-container/40 transition-all duration-150 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3";
            const indicator = c.querySelector('.shrink-0');
            indicator.className = "w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-primary";
            indicator.innerHTML = '<div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></div>';
            const textDesc = c.querySelector('p');
            textDesc.className = "font-body-sm text-body-sm text-outline leading-snug";
          });

          // Select current
          card.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
          card.className = "relative bg-[#F4EFFF] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border-2 border-primary-container cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ring-4 ring-primary-container/10";
          const indicator = card.querySelector('.shrink-0');
          indicator.className = "w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm";
          indicator.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-white text-[16px] font-bold" data-icon="check">check</span>';
          const textDesc = card.querySelector('p');
          textDesc.className = "font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant leading-snug font-normal";
        });
      });
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

<body class="bg-surface font-body-md text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-start" style="background-color: #F7F6FC;">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Shell (Max Width 480px) -->
<div class="w-full max-w-lg min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between bg-surface relative" style="background-color: #F7F6FC;">
<!-- Top Navigation Anchor (Shared Component: TopAppBar with 6-segment Indicator) -->
<header class="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur-md" style="background-color: #F7F6FC;">
<div class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-4 h-16 max-w-lg mx-auto">
<!-- Leading Action: Back -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95 text-on-surface">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Centered Header & Segmented Progress -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-1.5 flex-1 px-3"><span class="text-[14px] leading-5 font-semibold text-[#25243A]">Step 4 of 6</span>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-36">
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
</div></div>
<!-- Trailing Action: Skip -->
<button class="text-label-lg font-label-lg text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface px-2 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors duration-150 active:scale-95">
          Skip
        </button>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-3 pb-8 flex flex-col w-full max-w-lg mx-auto">
<!-- Section Header -->
<div class="mb-6">
<h1 class="text-headline-lg-mobile font-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface">
          How much daily practice works for you?
        </h1>
<p class="text-body-md font-body-md text-on-surface-variant mt-2">
          Consistent micro-habits lead to lasting score improvements.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Time Selection Cards -->
<div aria-label="Daily practice duration options" class="space-y-3" role="radiogroup">
<!-- Option 1: 10 min / day -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] transition-transform duration-150 hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/80">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">10 min / day</span>
<span class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Casual sprint</span>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Option 2: 15 min / day -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] transition-transform duration-150 hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/80">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">15 min / day</span>
<span class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Steady habit</span>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Option 3: 20 min / day (SELECTED) -->
<div aria-checked="true" class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border-2 border-primary-container flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer relative" style="background-color: #F5F3FF; border-color: #6558D3;">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">20 min / day</span>
<span class="bg-secondary-container text-on-surface text-label-sm font-label-sm px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full inline-flex items-center">
                Recommended
              </span>
</div>
<span class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Optimal progress pace</span>
</div>
<!-- Filled Primary Checkmark Indicator -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]" style="font-variation-settings: 'wght' 700;">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Option 4: 30 min / day -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] transition-transform duration-150 hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/80">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">30 min / day</span>
<span class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Intensive prep</span>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Option 5: 45 min / day -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.99] transition-transform duration-150 hover:bg-surface-container-lowest/80">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">45 min / day</span>
<span class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Fast-track focus</span>
</div>
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center">
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-transparent"></span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Reminder Card Section -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 mt-5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div>
<h2 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">Daily study reminder</h2>
<p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">Stay on track with a timely ping</p>
</div>
<!-- Toggle Switch ON -->
<label class="relative inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
<input checked="" class="sr-only peer" type="checkbox"/>
<div class="w-12 h-7 bg-surface-dim peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer peer-checked:after:translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:left-[2px] after:bg-surface-container-lowest after:border-outline-variant after:border after:rounded-full after:h-6 after:w-6 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-primary-container"></div>
</label>
</div>
<!-- Time Picker Row -->
<div class="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-outline-variant/20 flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-on-surface">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-surface-container-high flex items-center justify-center text-primary-container">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]" data-icon="notifications">notifications</span>
</div>
<span class="text-body-lg font-headline-sm text-on-surface font-semibold tracking-tight">07:30 PM</span>
</div>
<button class="text-label-md font-label-md text-primary font-bold hover:bg-surface-container-high px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full transition-colors active:scale-95">
            Change time
          </button>
</div>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom CTA Container -->
<footer class="sticky bottom-0 w-full bg-surface/95 backdrop-blur-md px-4 py-4 max-w-lg mx-auto" style="background-color: #F7F6FC;">
<button class="w-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary text-label-lg font-label-lg font-semibold py-4 rounded-[14px] shadow-sm hover:brightness-95 active:scale-[0.99] transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</footer>
</div>
</body></html>

<body class="min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between items-center selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-primary">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Container -->
<div class="w-full max-w-lg min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-between relative bg-[#F7F6FC]">
<!-- Header / Top Bar -->
<header class="w-full sticky top-0 z-30 bg-[#F7F6FC]/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<!-- Shared TopAppBar layout and anchors executed -->
<div class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-4 h-16 max-w-lg mx-auto">
<!-- Leading Icon -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container-high transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Centered Progress Group -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5 flex-1 px-2 w-36">
<span class="text-label-md font-label-md text-on-surface-variant font-semibold">Step 5 of 6</span>
<!-- 6-Segment Progress Bar -->
<div aria-valuemax="6" aria-valuemin="1" aria-valuenow="5" class="flex items-center gap-1 w-full" role="progressbar">
<!-- Segment 1 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 2 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 3 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 4 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 5 (Filled) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<!-- Segment 6 (Empty/Subtle) -->
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-surface-dim"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Trailing Action -->
<button class="h-10 px-3 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-label-lg font-label-lg font-semibold text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container-high hover:text-on-surface transition-colors active:scale-95 duration-150" type="button">
          Skip
        </button>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Area -->
<main class="flex-1 px-4 pt-3 pb-8 flex flex-col justify-start">
<!-- Hero Header -->
<div class="mb-6 space-y-1.5 text-left">
<h1 class="text-headline-lg-mobile font-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight">
          Which skills need the most focus?
        </h1>
<p class="text-body-md font-body-md text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed">
          Pick one or more to personalize your daily sprint.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- 2x2 Bento Selection Grid -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3.5 w-full">
<!-- 1. Listening (Unselected) -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-on-surface/[0.08] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 hover:shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">
<!-- Icon Badge Tinted -->
<div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-[#EEF4FE] flex items-center justify-center text-[#7BA7F7]">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]" data-icon="headphones">headphones</span>
</div>
<!-- Unselected Circle Indicator -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"></div>
</div>
<div class="mt-3">
<h2 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface mb-0.5">Listening</h2>
<p class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal leading-snug">Sections 1–4, Audio note-taking</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 2. Reading (Unselected) -->
<div aria-checked="false" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-on-surface/[0.08] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 hover:shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">
<!-- Icon Badge Tinted -->
<div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-[#EDF8F6] flex items-center justify-center text-[#70C2B4]">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]" data-icon="menu_book">menu_book</span>
</div>
<!-- Unselected Circle Indicator -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"></div>
</div>
<div class="mt-3">
<h2 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface mb-0.5">Reading</h2>
<p class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal leading-snug">Skimming, True/False/Not Given</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 3. Writing (SELECTED) -->
<div aria-checked="true" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-[#F5F3FF] border-2 border-primary-container shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">
<!-- Icon Badge Tinted -->
<div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-[#FEF4EF] flex items-center justify-center text-[#F69E7B]">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]" data-icon="edit_note">edit_note</span>
</div>
<!-- Selected Checkmark Circle -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill-icon text-white text-[15px]" data-icon="check" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-3">
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-0.5">
<h2 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">Writing</h2>
</div>
<p class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal leading-snug">Task 1 charts &amp; Task 2 essays</p>
</div>
</div>
<!-- 4. Speaking (SELECTED) -->
<div aria-checked="true" class="group relative min-h-[152px] p-4 rounded-[18px] bg-[#F5F3FF] border-2 border-primary-container shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)] flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]" role="checkbox" tabindex="0">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between w-full">
<!-- Icon Badge Tinted -->
<div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-full bg-[#F4EFFF] flex items-center justify-center text-[#A07BE6]">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]" data-icon="mic">mic</span>
</div>
<!-- Selected Checkmark Circle -->
<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill-icon text-white text-[15px]" data-icon="check" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mt-3">
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-0.5">
<h2 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface">Speaking</h2>
</div>
<p class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal leading-snug">Fluency, Lexical range &amp; Pronunciation</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Informative Subtext Banner -->
<div class="mt-6 p-3.5 bg-surface-container-lowest/80 rounded-[14px] border border-outline-variant/30 flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-secondary-container/40 flex items-center justify-center text-on-secondary-container flex-shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]" data-icon="tune">tune</span>
</div>
<p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">You can change these anytime in your study plan.</p>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Sticky Bottom CTA Section -->
<footer class="sticky bottom-0 w-full p-4 pt-3 pb-6 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md">
<div class="max-w-lg mx-auto">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container hover:bg-surface-tint active:scale-[0.99] text-white text-body-lg font-semibold rounded-[14px] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(101,88,211,0.25)] flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-150" type="button">
          Continue
        </button>
</div>
</footer>
</div>
<!-- Interactive State Toggle Script -->
<script>
    document.querySelectorAll('[role="checkbox"]').forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isChecked = card.getAttribute('aria-checked') === 'true';
        const indicator = card.querySelector('.rounded-full:last-child');
        
        if (isChecked) {
          card.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
          card.classList.remove('bg-[#F5F3FF]', 'border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)]');
          card.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'border', 'border-on-surface/[0.08]', 'shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]');
          
          indicator.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center transition-colors';
          indicator.innerHTML = '';
        } else {
          card.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
          card.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'border', 'border-on-surface/[0.08]', 'shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)]');
          card.classList.add('bg-[#F5F3FF]', 'border-2', 'border-primary-container', 'shadow-[0_6px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.12)]');
          
          indicator.className = 'w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs';
          indicator.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined fill-icon text-white text-[15px]" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">check</span>';
        }
      });
    });
  </script>
</body></html>

            "label-lg": ["Inter"]
          },
          "fontSize": {
            "headline-xl": ["36px", { "lineHeight": "44px", "letterSpacing": "-0.025em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "headline-lg": ["28px", { "lineHeight": "36px", "letterSpacing": "-0.02em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "label-md": ["12px", { "lineHeight": "16px", "letterSpacing": "0.02em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "headline-xl-mobile": ["28px", { "lineHeight": "36px", "letterSpacing": "-0.02em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "label-sm": ["11px", { "lineHeight": "14px", "letterSpacing": "0.04em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "headline-lg-mobile": ["24px", { "lineHeight": "32px", "letterSpacing": "-0.015em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "body-md": ["14px", { "lineHeight": "22px", "letterSpacing": "0em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "body-lg": ["16px", { "lineHeight": "26px", "letterSpacing": "-0.005em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "body-sm": ["13px", { "lineHeight": "18px", "letterSpacing": "0.005em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "headline-md": ["20px", { "lineHeight": "28px", "letterSpacing": "-0.015em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "headline-sm": ["18px", { "lineHeight": "24px", "letterSpacing": "-0.01em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "label-lg": ["14px", { "lineHeight": "20px", "letterSpacing": "0.01em", "fontWeight": "600" }]
          }
        }
      }
    }
  </script>
</head>
<body class="bg-[#F7F6FC] text-[#25243A] font-body-md text-body-md antialiased min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-primary">
<!-- Main Viewport Shell: Mobile-first native max-w-md centered -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col min-h-screen bg-[#F7F6FC] relative pb-28">
<!-- Top Navigation & Stage Progress Header -->
<header class="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-[#F7F6FC]/90 backdrop-blur-md px-margin pt-space-sm pb-space-sm">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
<!-- Back Navigation Button -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all text-[#25243A]" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Step Indicator & Progress Segments -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 max-w-[170px]">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-[#25243A] tracking-tight">Step 6 of 6</span>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-[144px]">
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Skip Placement Action -->
<button class="text-right px-2 py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-primary active:scale-95 transition-all font-label-md text-label-md font-semibold" type="button">
          Skip
        </button>
</div>
<!-- Question Sub-Progress Tracker -->
<div class="mt-4 px-1 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[#25243A]">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-semibold text-primary">Question 3 of 8</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Grammar &amp; Syntax</span>
</div>
<div class="w-full bg-[#E5E2F3] h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
<div class="bg-[#6558D3] h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300" style="width: 37.5%;"></div>
</div>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="px-margin pt-2 flex flex-col gap-4 flex-1">
<!-- Advisory Helper Note Banner -->
<div class="flex items-start gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-[14px] bg-[#EDEBF7] border border-outline-variant/30 text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] text-primary flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">info</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm leading-snug">
          This helps us recommend a starting point. It is not an official level.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Question Card Prompt -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-5 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex flex-col gap-3">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-primary">Sentence Completion</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium">Task 2 Prep</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-[#25243A] leading-snug">
          Choose the correct sentence structure:
        </h1>
<div class="p-3.5 rounded-[12px] bg-[#F7F6FC] border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.06)]">
<p class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A] leading-relaxed">
            “By the time the conference ended, the delegates <span class="inline-block px-3 py-0.5 mx-1 font-semibold text-primary border-b-2 border-primary bg-primary/10 rounded-sm">______</span> several key environmental agreements.”
          </p>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Multiple Choice Options -->
<section aria-label="Answer Options" class="flex flex-col gap-3">
<!-- Option A: SELECTED & CORRECT STATE -->
<div class="relative bg-[#EBF8F1] border-2 border-[#38A169] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] flex items-center justify-between transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#38A169] flex items-center justify-center text-white flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg font-bold text-[#25243A]">had finalized</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-[#286A5E] font-medium">Past Perfect</span>
</div>
</div>
<span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#38A169] text-white font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold tracking-wide uppercase">
            Correct
          </span>
</div>
<!-- Option B: Unselected -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex items-center justify-between opacity-85 hover:border-[#6558D3]/50 transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant flex-shrink-0">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">B</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A]">have finalized</span>
</div>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border border-outline-variant"></div>
</div>
<!-- Option C: Unselected -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex items-center justify-between opacity-85 hover:border-[#6558D3]/50 transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant flex-shrink-0">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">C</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A]">were finalizing</span>
</div>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border border-outline-variant"></div>
</div>
<!-- Option D: Unselected -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex items-center justify-between opacity-85 hover:border-[#6558D3]/50 transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant flex-shrink-0">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">D</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A]">would finalize</span>
</div>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border border-outline-variant"></div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Pedagogical Explanation Card -->
<section class="bg-[#EBF8F1] border border-[#38A169]/30 rounded-[14px] p-3.5 flex items-start gap-3 shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] text-[#286A5E] flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">lightbulb</span>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-[#286A5E]">Grammar Breakdown</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-[#25243A] leading-relaxed">
<strong class="font-semibold">Explanation:</strong> Past Perfect (<em class="italic">‘had finalized’</em>) is required because the action was completed before another past event (<em class="italic">‘the conference ended’</em>). Common in IELTS Academic Task 2 writing.
          </p>
</div>
</section>
</main>
<!-- Bottom Action Shell (Fixed bottom for focused task flow) -->
<div class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md px-margin pt-3 pb-6 max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col items-center gap-2.5">
<!-- Sticky Primary Action Button -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-[#6558D3] hover:bg-primary text-white font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.25)] active:scale-[0.98] active:brightness-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
<!-- Subordinate Alternative Action -->
<button class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant hover:text-[#25243A] active:scale-95 transition-all py-1" type="button">
        Skip placement
      </button>
</div>
</div>
</body></html>

<!-- Bandwise — Your Plan -->

<body class="bg-background text-on-surface antialiased min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between selection:bg-secondary-container selection:text-on-secondary-fixed">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Container capped at max-w-md -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col flex-1 pb-36">
<!-- Top Navigation Bar (Shared Component Contract) -->
<header class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-margin py-space-sm max-w-md mx-auto sticky top-0 z-50 shadow-sm bg-surface">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-transform duration-150 text-primary" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm font-bold text-primary">Bandwise</span>
</div>
<button aria-label="Help and Info" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-transform duration-150 text-primary" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="help_outline">help_outline</span>
</button>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="px-margin pt-space-md flex-1 flex flex-col gap-4">
<!-- Header Title Section -->
<section class="space-y-1">
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary" data-icon="auto_awesome" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">auto_awesome</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm tracking-wide">AI ROADMAP GENERATED</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-xl-mobile text-headline-xl-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight pt-1">
          Your Study Plan is Ready!
        </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          Tailored to your goal, schedule, and exam date.
        </p>
</section>
<!-- Recommended Starting Point Card -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-primary-container/25 relative overflow-hidden transition-all hover:shadow-md">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2.5">
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" data-icon="flag">flag</span>
            Recommended Starting Point
          </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-primary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]" data-icon="play_arrow" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">play_arrow</span>
</span>
</div>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface mb-1 leading-snug">
          Intermediate · Unit 2: Academic Vocabulary &amp; Argument Structure
        </h2>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-tertiary" data-icon="verified">verified</span>
          Based on your diagnostic check and target band.
        </p>
</section>
<!-- Plan Summary Card (2x2 Key Plan Metrics) -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-on-surface/5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-surface-container">
<span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface">Plan Overview</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant bg-surface-container-low px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">8 Weeks Track</span>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
<!-- Metric 1: Target Goal -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Target Goal</span>
<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-container text-primary font-label-sm text-[10px]">Good user</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-1 mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-primary font-bold">Band 7.0</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-secondary"></span> Diagnostic: 6.0
            </span>
</div>
<!-- Metric 2: Exam Date -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Exam Date</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-on-surface-variant" data-icon="calendar_today">calendar_today</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface block">Nov 22, 2025</span>
</div>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-[11px] text-tertiary mt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[13px]" data-icon="timelapse">timelapse</span> 8 weeks left
            </span>
</div>
<!-- Metric 3: Daily Commitment -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Commitment</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-on-surface-variant" data-icon="schedule">schedule</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface block">20 min / day</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[13px] text-primary" data-icon="alarm">alarm</span> 7:30 PM reminder
            </span>
</div>
<!-- Metric 4: Priority Skills Focus -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Priority Focus</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary" data-icon="target">target</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1 mt-1">
<div class="inline-flex items-center px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#FEF4EF] text-[#C45E3B] font-bold">
                Writing
              </div>
<div class="inline-flex items-center px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#F4EFFF] text-[#6558D3] font-bold">
                Speaking
              </div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Skills Breakdown Chips -->
<div class="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-surface-container">
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant block mb-2">Targeted modules:</span>
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#FEF4EF] text-[#C45E3B]">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#F69E7B]"></span>
              Writing: Task 1 &amp; Task 2
            </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#F4EFFF] text-[#6558D3]">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#A07BE6]"></span>
              Speaking: Fluency &amp; Lexical
            </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#EDF8F6] text-[#286A5E] opacity-90">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#70C2B4]"></span>
              Reading
            </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#EEF4FE] text-[#3667B2] opacity-90">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#7BA7F7]"></span>
              Listening
            </span>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Weekly Schedule Preview -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-on-surface/5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">Weekly Routine</h3>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed text-label-sm font-label-sm">
            4 days / week
          </span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-3">
          Micro-learning sessions calibrated around optimal spaced repetition.
        </p>
<!-- 7-day Horizontal Row -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 text-center">
<!-- Mon: Active -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Mon</span>
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span>
</div>
<!-- Tue: Active -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Tue</span>
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span>
</div>
<!-- Wed: Rest -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low opacity-60">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Wed</span>
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline-variant my-1.5"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">Rest</span>
</div>
<!-- Thu: Active -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Thu</span>
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span>
</div>
<!-- Fri: Rest -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low opacity-60">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Fri</span>
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline-variant my-1.5"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">Rest</span>
</div>
<!-- Sat: Active -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Sat</span>
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span>
</div>
<!-- Sun: Rest -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container-low opacity-60">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Sun</span>
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-outline-variant my-1.5"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">Rest</span>
</div>
</div>
</section>
</main>
<!-- Docked Action Footer -->
<footer class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full z-50 bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-md px-margin py-3 shadow-[0_-4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border-none">
<div class="max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary active:scale-[0.98] transition-all rounded-[14px] text-on-primary font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-md" type="button">
<span>Start learning</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
<button class="w-full py-2.5 bg-transparent hover:bg-surface-container text-primary-container active:scale-[0.98] transition-all rounded-[14px] font-label-lg text-label-lg font-medium text-center" type="button">
          Adjust plan
        </button>
</div>
</footer>
</div>
</body></html>

            "on-secondary-fixed": "#141f00",

          },

          },

    .material-symbols-outlined {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 0, 'wght' 400, 'GRAD' 0, 'opsz' 24;
      display: inline-block;
      vertical-align: middle;
