    }
    .material-symbols-outlined[data-weight="fill"] {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="bg-[#F7F6FC] text-on-surface antialiased min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between selection:bg-primary-container selection:text-white">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Container -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col flex-1 pb-space-xl">
<!-- Top Bar Navigation (Linear Transactional Flow, No global bottom shell needed per mandate) -->
<header class="w-full px-margin py-space-sm flex justify-between items-center sticky top-0 z-50 bg-[#F7F6FC]/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<!-- Back Chevron -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Secondary Action Link -->
<a class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-primary-container font-semibold hover:opacity-80 active:scale-95 transition-all py-1.5 px-3 rounded-lg" href="#login">
        Log in
      </a>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="px-margin flex-1 flex flex-col pt-space-xs">
<!-- Headline & Subtitle -->
<div class="mb-space-lg">
<!-- Minimal Band 7+ target badge -->
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-primary-container font-label-sm text-label-sm mb-space-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" data-weight="fill">school</span>
<span>IELTS Academic Prep</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight mb-space-xs">
          Create your account
        </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          Save your IELTS study plan and sync your progress across devices.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Social Sign-In Button -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-white border border-[#25243A]/10 hover:border-outline-variant hover:bg-surface-bright rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center gap-3 shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] active:scale-[0.99] transition-all mb-space-md" type="button">
<!-- Google Logo SVG -->
<svg aria-hidden="true" class="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0" viewbox="0 0 24 24">
<path d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z" fill="#4285F4"></path>
<path d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z" fill="#34A853"></path>
<path d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.06H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.94l2.85-2.22.81-.63z" fill="#FBBC05"></path>
<path d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.06l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.52 6.16-4.52z" fill="#EA4335"></path>
</svg>
<span class="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface">Continue with Google</span>
</button>
<!-- Divider -->
<div class="relative flex items-center justify-center my-space-xs mb-space-md">
<div class="w-full border-t border-[#25243A]/10"></div>
<span class="absolute bg-[#F7F6FC] px-3 font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant/80">
          or continue with email
        </span>
</div>
<!-- Registration Form -->
<form class="space-y-space-md" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();">
<!-- Field 1: Full Name -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface" for="fullName">
            Full Name
          </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] px-4 rounded-[14px] bg-white border border-[#25243A]/12 text-on-surface placeholder:text-outline-variant font-body-md text-body-md focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary-container/15 focus:outline-none transition-all shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(37,36,58,0.03)]" id="fullName" name="fullName" placeholder="Alex Morgan" type="text"/>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Field 2: Email address with Inline Validation Error -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface" for="email">
            Email address
          </label>
<div class="relative">
<input aria-describedby="email-error" aria-invalid="true" class="w-full h-[52px] px-4 rounded-[14px] bg-[#FDF0F0] border-2 border-[#E5484D] text-[#25243A] font-body-md text-body-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#E5484D]/15 transition-all shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(229,72,77,0.05)]" id="email" name="email" type="email" value="alex.morgan@domain"/>
<div class="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#E5484D] pointer-events-none flex items-center">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-weight="fill">error</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Inline Error Text -->
<p class="text-[#E5484D] text-xs font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 tracking-tight" id="email-error">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] leading-none">warning</span>
<span>Please enter a valid email address (e.g. name@example.com)</span>
</p>
</div>
<!-- Field 3: Password with Visibility Toggle & Strength Meter -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface" for="password">
            Password
          </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] pl-4 pr-12 rounded-[14px] bg-white border border-[#25243A]/12 text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary-container/15 focus:outline-none transition-all shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(37,36,58,0.03)]" id="password" name="password" type="password" value="••••••••••"/>
<button aria-label="Toggle password visibility" class="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface p-1 rounded transition-colors" id="togglePassword" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" id="eyeIcon">visibility</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Password Strength Indicator -->
<div class="mt-1 space-y-1.5">
<div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 w-full">
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#38A169]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#38A169]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#38A169]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#25243A]/10"></div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="text-xs font-medium text-[#38A169] flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" data-weight="fill">check_circle</span>
                Strong password
              </span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">8+ chars required</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Terms & Privacy Checkbox -->
<div class="pt-space-xs">
<label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer select-none">
<div class="relative flex items-center mt-0.5">
<input checked="" class="peer sr-only" id="terms" type="checkbox"/>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-md border-2 border-primary-container bg-primary-container flex items-center justify-center text-white peer-checked:bg-primary-container peer-focus:ring-2 peer-focus:ring-primary-container/30 transition-all shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] font-bold">check</span>
</div>
</div>
<span class="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-normal">
              I agree to the <a class="text-primary-container font-semibold hover:underline" href="#terms">Terms of Service</a> and <a class="text-primary-container font-semibold hover:underline" href="#privacy">Privacy Policy</a>.
            </span>
</label>
</div>
<!-- Primary CTA Button -->
<div class="pt-space-xs">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container text-white font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold rounded-[14px] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(101,88,211,0.25)] hover:bg-primary active:scale-[0.99] active:brightness-95 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2" type="submit">
<span>Create Account</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</form>
<!-- Bottom Note / Footer Redirect -->
<div class="mt-space-lg text-center pb-space-sm">
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          Already have an account? 
          <a class="text-primary-container font-semibold hover:underline ml-1" href="#login">
            Log in
          </a>
</p>
</div>
</main>
</div>
<script>
    // Lightweight password visibility toggle interaction
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
    const eyeIcon = document.getElementById('eyeIcon');

    if (toggleBtn && passwordInput && eyeIcon) {
      toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isPassword = passwordInput.getAttribute('type') === 'password';
        passwordInput.setAttribute('type', isPassword ? 'text' : 'password');
        eyeIcon.textContent = isPassword ? 'visibility_off' : 'visibility';
      });
    }
  </script>
</body></html>

    .material-symbols-outlined {

<body class="bg-background text-on-surface antialiased min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Container capped at max-w-md -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md mx-auto min-h-screen flex flex-col px-margin pb-8 pt-safe">
<!-- Top Bar Component (Transactional Linear State: Shell nav suppressed per Semantic Shell Mandate) -->
<header class="flex justify-between items-center w-full py-space-sm mb-2 sticky top-0 z-40 bg-background/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-secondary-container"></span>
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">Bandwise</span>
</div>
<a class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-primary-container hover:text-primary font-semibold transition-colors py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-lg active:scale-95" href="#">
        Sign up
      </a>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Area -->
<main class="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center">
<!-- Heading Cluster -->
<div class="mt-2 mb-6">
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container-high text-primary font-label-sm text-label-sm mb-3">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]" data-icon="school">school</span>
<span>Target Band 7.5+ Engine</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-xl-mobile text-headline-xl-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight">
          Welcome back
        </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant mt-1.5">
          Log in to continue your IELTS prep sprint.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Main Surface Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-5 custom-shadow border border-slate-100 ambient-halo">
<!-- Error Alert Banner (Active Error State) -->
<div class="bg-[#FDF0F0] border border-[#E5484D]/40 rounded-[14px] p-3.5 mb-5 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all" role="alert">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[#E5484D] shrink-0 text-[20px]" data-icon="error">error</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm font-medium text-[#E5484D] leading-snug">
            Email or password is incorrect. Please try again.
          </p>
</div>
<!-- Social Auth: Continue with Google -->
<button class="w-full bg-surface-container-lowest hover:bg-surface-container-low active:scale-[0.99] border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] py-3.5 px-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 transition-all duration-150" type="button">
<svg class="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" fill="none" viewbox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<path d="M23.745 12.27c0-.7-.06-1.4-.19-2.07H12v4.51h6.6c-.29 1.52-1.14 2.82-2.4 3.68v3.05h3.88c2.27-2.09 3.66-5.17 3.66-9.17z" fill="#4285F4"></path>
<path d="M12 24c3.24 0 5.95-1.08 7.93-2.91l-3.88-3.05c-1.08.72-2.45 1.16-4.05 1.16-3.12 0-5.77-2.1-6.72-4.93H1.25v3.15C3.26 21.36 7.33 24 12 24z" fill="#34A853"></path>
<path d="M5.28 14.27c-.25-.72-.38-1.49-.38-2.27s.13-1.55.38-2.27V6.58H1.25C.45 8.17 0 9.97 0 12s.45 3.83 1.25 5.42l4.03-3.15z" fill="#FBBC05"></path>
<path d="M12 4.75c1.77 0 3.35.61 4.6 1.8l3.42-3.42C17.95 1.19 15.24 0 12 0 7.33 0 3.26 2.64 1.25 6.58l4.03 3.15c.95-2.83 3.6-4.98 6.72-4.98z" fill="#EA4335"></path>
</svg>
<span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface font-semibold">
            Continue with Google
          </span>
</button>
<!-- Divider -->
<div class="relative flex items-center justify-center my-5">
<div class="w-full border-t border-slate-200"></div>
<span class="absolute px-3 bg-surface-container-lowest font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal">
            or log in with email
          </span>
</div>
<!-- Form Fields -->
<form class="space-y-4" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();">
<!-- Email Input -->
<div class="space-y-1.5">
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant" for="email">
              Email address
            </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] px-4 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20 transition-all placeholder:text-outline" id="email" placeholder="Enter your email" type="email" value="alex.morgan@example.com"/>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant text-[20px] pointer-events-none" data-icon="mail">mail</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Password Input -->
<div class="space-y-1.5">
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant" for="password">
              Password
            </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] px-4 pr-11 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface tracking-widest focus:outline-none focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20 transition-all" id="password" type="password" value="••••••••••••"/>
<button aria-label="Toggle password visibility" class="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface p-1 rounded-md transition-colors" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="visibility">visibility</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Options Row: Remember Me & Forgot Password -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
<label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
<input checked="" class="w-4 h-4 rounded text-primary-container focus:ring-primary-container/20 border-slate-300" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Remember me</span>
</label>
<a class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-primary-container hover:text-primary font-medium transition-colors" href="#">
              Forgot password?
            </a>
</div>
<!-- Primary CTA Button (LOADING STATE) -->
<div class="pt-2">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container text-on-primary font-label-lg text-label-lg rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-wait opacity-90 shadow-sm active:translate-y-[1px] transition-all" disabled="" type="submit">
<!-- Circular Animated Spinner -->
<svg class="animate-spin h-5 w-5 text-on-primary" fill="none" viewbox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3"></circle>
<path class="opacity-75" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z" fill="currentColor"></path>
</svg>
<span>Logging in...</span>
</button>
</div>
</form>
</div>
<!-- Quick Band Goal Badge for Encouragement -->
<div class="mt-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-secondary-fixed-dim"></span>
<span>Synchronizing with Academic Sprint Tracker</span>
</div>
</main>
<!-- Bottom Footer Anchor -->
<footer class="pt-6 pb-2 text-center">
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
        Don't have an account? 
        <a class="text-primary-container hover:text-primary font-semibold transition-colors underline-offset-4 hover:underline" href="#">
          Sign up
        </a>
</p>
</footer>
</div>
</body></html>

    .material-symbols-outlined {
      font-family: 'Material Symbols Outlined';
      font-weight: normal;
      font-style: normal;
      font-size: 24px;
      line-height: 1;
      letter-spacing: normal;
      text-transform: none;
      display: inline-block;
      white-space: nowrap;
      word-wrap: normal;
      direction: ltr;

<body class="bg-background text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-start items-center selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed">
<!-- Mobile Container Shell -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-surface relative pb-8">
<!-- TopAppBar (from Shared Components JSON: docked small top app bar, back button leading, help icon trailing) -->
<header class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-margin py-space-sm max-w-md mx-auto sticky top-0 z-50 shadow-sm bg-surface">
<button aria-label="Go back" class="p-2 -ml-2 rounded-full text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all duration-150 flex items-center justify-center" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<div class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm font-bold text-primary tracking-tight">
        Bandwise
      </div>
<button aria-label="Help &amp; FAQ" class="p-2 -mr-2 rounded-full text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all duration-150 flex items-center justify-center" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">help_outline</span>
</button>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="flex-1 px-margin pt-space-md flex flex-col">
<!-- Academic Goal Pill Tag -->
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 self-start px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container-high text-primary mb-3">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">lock_reset</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm tracking-wide">ACCOUNT RECOVERY</span>
</div>
<!-- Header Section -->
<div class="mb-5">
<h1 class="text-headline-lg-mobile font-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight mb-2">
          Reset your password
        </h1>
<p class="text-body-md font-body-md text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed">
          Enter the email linked to your Bandwise account and we'll send you reset instructions.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Compact Input Field Form State (Pre-filled / Sent State) -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 mb-4 transition-all">
<label class="block text-label-md font-label-md text-on-surface-variant mb-1.5" for="email">
          Email address
        </label>
<div class="relative flex items-center">
<input class="w-full h-[50px] px-4 rounded-[14px] bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md border border-outline-variant/40 focus:outline-none cursor-default pr-10" id="email" readonly="" type="email" value="alex.morgan@example.com"/>
<div class="absolute right-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-tertiary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check_circle</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- SUCCESS STATE CARD (Prominently displayed) -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-6 shadow-sm border border-[#38A169]/30 text-center relative overflow-hidden transition-all">
<!-- Subtle gamified success glow aura -->
<div class="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-32 h-32 bg-[#EBF8F1] rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none opacity-60"></div>
<!-- Status Icon Badge -->
<div class="w-14 h-14 mx-auto mb-3.5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#EBF8F1] text-[#38A169] shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[28px]" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">mark_email_read</span>
</div>
<!-- Headline & Feedback Copy -->
<h2 class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm text-on-surface mb-2">
          Check your inbox
        </h2>
<p class="text-body-md font-body-md text-on-surface-variant mb-4 leading-relaxed">
          We sent a password reset link to <span class="font-semibold text-on-surface">alex.morgan@example.com</span>. Check your spam folder if it doesn't arrive within 2 minutes.
        </p>
<!-- Divider line subtle -->
<div class="w-full h-px bg-surface-container mb-4"></div>
<!-- Resend Timer Block -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
<span class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Didn't receive the email?</span>
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-surface-container px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" id="resend-badge">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-on-surface-variant">schedule</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm" id="countdown-text">Resend link in 0:48</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Action Buttons Container -->
<div class="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3">
<!-- Primary App Action CTA -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container text-on-primary rounded-[14px] font-semibold text-label-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm hover:brightness-105 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-150" onclick="openMailClient()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">mail</span>
<span>Open email app</span>
</button>
<!-- Text Action / Secondary Escape Navigation -->
<a class="text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface font-label-lg text-body-md text-center py-2 transition-colors duration-150 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1" href="#">
<span>Back to Log in</span>
</a>
</div>
<!-- IELTS Academic Support Micro-Callout -->
<div class="mt-auto pt-6 pb-2 text-center">
<p class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-outline">
          Need urgent exam prep access? <a class="text-primary font-semibold underline underline-offset-2" href="#">Contact Bandwise Support</a>
</p>
</div>
</main>
</div>
<!-- Interactive Timer & Feedback Script -->
<script>
    let secondsLeft = 48;
    const countdownElement = document.getElementById('countdown-text');
    const resendBadge = document.getElementById('resend-badge');

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (secondsLeft > 0) {
        secondsLeft--;
        const formatted = secondsLeft < 10 ? `0:0${secondsLeft}` : `0:${secondsLeft}`;
        countdownElement.textContent = `Resend link in ${formatted}`;
      } else {
        clearInterval(timer);
        resendBadge.innerHTML = `
          <button type="button" onclick="triggerResend()" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-primary font-bold hover:underline">
            <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">refresh</span>
            <span>Resend reset email now</span>
          </button>
        `;
        resendBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-primary-fixed px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-on-primary-fixed transition-all cursor-pointer";
      }
    }, 1000);

    function triggerResend() {
      resendBadge.innerHTML = `
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-tertiary">check</span>
        <span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-tertiary">Email resent!</span>
      `;
      resendBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#EBF8F1] px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-[#38A169]";
    }

    function openMailClient() {
      window.location.href = "mailto:";
    }
  </script>
</body></html>

<body class="bg-[#F7F6FC] text-[#25243A] font-body-md text-body-md antialiased min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-primary">
<!-- Main Viewport Shell: Mobile-first native max-w-md centered -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col min-h-screen bg-[#F7F6FC] relative pb-28">
<!-- Top Navigation & Stage Progress Header -->
<header class="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-[#F7F6FC]/90 backdrop-blur-md px-margin pt-space-sm pb-space-sm">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
<!-- Back Navigation Button -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all text-[#25243A]" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Step Indicator & Progress Segments -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 max-w-[170px]">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-[#25243A] tracking-tight">Step 6 of 6</span>
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-[144px]">
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#6558D3]"></div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Skip Placement Action -->
</div>
<!-- Question Sub-Progress Tracker -->
<div class="mt-4 px-1 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[#25243A]">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-semibold text-primary">Question 3 of 8</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Grammar &amp; Syntax</span>
</div>
<div class="w-full bg-[#E5E2F3] h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
<div class="bg-[#6558D3] h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300" style="width: 37.5%;"></div>
</div>
</div>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="px-margin pt-2 flex flex-col gap-4 flex-1">
<!-- Advisory Helper Note Banner -->
<div class="flex items-start gap-2.5 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-[14px] bg-[#EDEBF7] border border-outline-variant/30 text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] text-primary flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">info</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm leading-snug">
          This helps us recommend a starting point. It is not an official level.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Question Card Prompt -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-5 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex flex-col gap-3">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-primary">Sentence Completion</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-[#25243A] leading-snug">
          Choose the correct sentence structure:
        </h1>
<div class="p-3.5 rounded-[12px] bg-[#F7F6FC] border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.06)]">
<p class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A] leading-relaxed">
            “By the time the conference ended, the delegates <span class="inline-block px-3 py-0.5 mx-1 font-semibold text-primary border-b-2 border-primary bg-primary/10 rounded-sm">______</span> several key environmental agreements.”
          </p>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Multiple Choice Options -->
<section aria-label="Answer Options" class="flex flex-col gap-3">
<!-- Option A: SELECTED & CORRECT STATE -->
<div class="relative bg-[#EBF8F1] border-2 border-[#38A169] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] flex items-center justify-between transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#38A169] flex items-center justify-center text-white flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">check</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg font-bold text-[#25243A]">had finalized</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-[#286A5E] font-medium">Past Perfect</span>
</div>
</div>
<span class="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-[#38A169] text-white font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold tracking-wide uppercase">
            Correct
          </span>
</div>
<!-- Option B: Unselected -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex items-center justify-between opacity-85 hover:border-[#6558D3]/50 transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant flex-shrink-0">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">B</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A]">have finalized</span>
</div>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border border-outline-variant"></div>
</div>
<!-- Option C: Unselected -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex items-center justify-between opacity-85 hover:border-[#6558D3]/50 transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant flex-shrink-0">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">C</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A]">were finalizing</span>
</div>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border border-outline-variant"></div>
</div>
<!-- Option D: Unselected -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest border border-[rgba(37,36,58,0.08)] rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] flex items-center justify-between opacity-85 hover:border-[#6558D3]/50 transition-all cursor-pointer">
<div class="flex items-center gap-3">
<div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 border-outline-variant flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant flex-shrink-0">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">D</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-lg text-body-lg text-[#25243A]">would finalize</span>
</div>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border border-outline-variant"></div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Pedagogical Explanation Card -->
<section class="bg-[#EBF8F1] border border-[#38A169]/30 rounded-[14px] p-3.5 flex items-start gap-3 shadow-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] text-[#286A5E] flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">lightbulb</span>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold text-[#286A5E]">Grammar Breakdown</span>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-[#25243A] leading-relaxed">
<strong class="font-semibold">Explanation:</strong> Past Perfect (<em class="italic">‘had finalized’</em>) is required because the action was completed before another past event (<em class="italic">‘the conference ended’</em>). Common in IELTS Academic Task 2 writing.
          </p>
</div>
</section>
</main>
<!-- Bottom Action Shell (Fixed bottom for focused task flow) -->
<div class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50 bg-[#F7F6FC]/95 backdrop-blur-md px-margin pt-3 pb-6 max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col items-center gap-2.5">
<!-- Sticky Primary Action Button -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-[#6558D3] hover:bg-primary text-white font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(101,88,211,0.25)] active:scale-[0.98] active:brightness-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span>Continue</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
<!-- Subordinate Alternative Action -->
<button class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant hover:text-[#25243A] active:scale-95 transition-all py-1" type="button">
        Skip placement
      </button>
</div>
</div>
</body></html>

<!-- Mobile Viewport Container capped at max-w-md -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col flex-1 pb-36">
<!-- Top Navigation Bar (Shared Component Contract) -->
<header class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-margin py-space-sm max-w-md mx-auto sticky top-0 z-50 shadow-sm bg-surface">
<div class="flex items-center gap-2">
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-transform duration-150 text-primary" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<span class="text-headline-sm font-headline-sm font-bold text-primary">Bandwise</span>
</div>
<button aria-label="Help and Info" class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-transform duration-150 text-primary" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="help_outline">help_outline</span>
</button>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="px-margin pt-space-md flex-1 flex flex-col gap-4">
<!-- Header Title Section -->
<section class="space-y-1">
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary" data-icon="auto_awesome" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">auto_awesome</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm tracking-wide">Plan ready</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-xl-mobile text-headline-xl-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight pt-1">
          Your Study Plan is Ready!
        </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          Tailored to your goal, schedule, and exam date.
        </p>
</section>
<!-- Recommended Starting Point Card -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-primary-container/25 relative overflow-hidden transition-all hover:shadow-md">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-2.5">
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" data-icon="flag">flag</span>
            Recommended Starting Point
          </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-primary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]" data-icon="play_arrow" data-weight="fill" style="font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;">play_arrow</span>
</span>
</div>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface mb-1 leading-snug">
          Intermediate · Unit 2: Academic Vocabulary &amp; Argument Structure
        </h2>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-tertiary" data-icon="verified">verified</span>
          Based on your diagnostic check and target band.
        </p>
</section>
<!-- Plan Summary Card (2x2 Key Plan Metrics) -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-on-surface/5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 mb-3 border-b border-surface-container">
<span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface">Plan Overview</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant bg-surface-container-low px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">8 Weeks Track</span>
</div>
<div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
<!-- Metric 1: Target Goal -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Target Goal</span>
<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-surface-container text-primary font-label-sm text-[10px]">Good user</span>
</div>
<div class="flex items-baseline gap-1 mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-primary font-bold">Band 7.0</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-secondary"></span> Starting level: Intermediate (recommendation)</span>
</div>
<!-- Metric 2: Exam Date -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Exam Date</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-on-surface-variant" data-icon="calendar_today">calendar_today</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface block">Dec 8, 2026</span>
</div>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-label-sm text-[11px] text-tertiary mt-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[13px]" data-icon="timelapse">timelapse</span> 8 weeks left
            </span>
</div>
<!-- Metric 3: Daily Commitment -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Commitment</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-on-surface-variant" data-icon="schedule">schedule</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-1">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface block">20 min / day</span>
</div>
<span class="font-body-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[13px] text-primary" data-icon="alarm">alarm</span> 7:30 PM reminder
            </span>
</div>
<!-- Metric 4: Priority Skills Focus -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">Priority Focus</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-secondary" data-icon="target">target</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1 mt-1">
<div class="inline-flex items-center px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#FEF4EF] text-[#C45E3B] font-bold">
                Writing
              </div>
<div class="inline-flex items-center px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#F4EFFF] text-[#6558D3] font-bold">
                Speaking
              </div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Skills Breakdown Chips -->
<div class="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-surface-container">
<span class="font-label-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant block mb-2">Targeted modules:</span>
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#FEF4EF] text-[#C45E3B]">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#F69E7B]"></span>
              Writing: Task 1 &amp; Task 2
            </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#F4EFFF] text-[#6558D3]">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#A07BE6]"></span>
              Speaking: Fluency &amp; Lexical
            </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#EDF8F6] text-[#286A5E] opacity-90">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#70C2B4]"></span>
              Reading
            </span>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-label-sm bg-[#EEF4FE] text-[#3667B2] opacity-90">
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#7BA7F7]"></span>
              Listening
            </span>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Weekly Schedule Preview -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-on-surface/5">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">Weekly Routine</h3>
<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed text-label-sm font-label-sm">7 days / week</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-3">
          Micro-learning sessions calibrated around optimal spaced repetition.
        </p>
<!-- 7-day Horizontal Row -->
<div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 text-center"><div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-primary text-on-primary shadow-sm border border-primary"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold text-on-primary">Mon</span><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-on-primary my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-on-primary font-bold">20m</span></div><div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Tue</span><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span></div><div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Wed</span><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span></div><div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Thu</span><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span></div><div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Fri</span><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span></div><div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Sat</span><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span></div><div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl bg-surface-container border border-primary/20"><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface">Sun</span><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container my-1.5 shadow-sm"></span><span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-primary font-bold">20m</span></div></div>
</section>
</main>
<!-- Docked Action Footer -->
<footer class="fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full z-50 bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-md px-margin py-3 shadow-[0_-4px_16px_rgba(37,36,58,0.06)] border-none">
<div class="max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary active:scale-[0.98] transition-all rounded-[14px] text-on-primary font-label-lg text-label-lg font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-md" type="button">
<span>Start learning</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="arrow_forward">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
<button class="w-full py-2.5 bg-transparent hover:bg-surface-container text-primary-container active:scale-[0.98] transition-all rounded-[14px] font-label-lg text-label-lg font-medium text-center" type="button">
          Adjust plan
        </button>
</div>
</footer>
</div>
</body></html>

<!-- Bandwise — Create Account -->
<!DOCTYPE html>

<html class="h-full" lang="en"><head>
<meta charset="utf-8"/>
<meta content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" name="viewport"/>
<title>Bandwise — Create Account</title>
<!-- Google Fonts: Inter -->
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" rel="preconnect"/>
<link crossorigin="" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" rel="preconnect"/>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/>
<!-- Material Symbols Outlined -->
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&amp;display=swap" rel="stylesheet"/>
<!-- Tailwind CSS -->
<script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com?plugins=forms,container-queries"></script>
<!-- Theme Configuration -->
<script id="tailwind-config">
    tailwind.config = {
      darkMode: "class",
      theme: {
        extend: {
          "colors": {

            "outline-variant": "#c8c4d6",
            "on-primary-container": "#eee9ff",
            "primary-fixed-dim": "#c6bfff",
            "tertiary-fixed": "#9ff2e3",
            "on-surface-variant": "#474553",
            "on-tertiary": "#ffffff",
            "on-secondary-container": "#576b2c",
            "surface-dim": "#dad7f4",
            "surface-tint": "#594bc6",
            "on-secondary": "#ffffff",
            "on-error": "#ffffff",
            "inverse-primary": "#c6bfff",
            "error": "#ba1a1a",
            "tertiary-fixed-dim": "#83d5c7",
            "on-surface": "#1a192f",
            "on-background": "#1a192f",
            "surface-container-low": "#f6f2ff",
            "surface-container": "#efebff",
            "outline": "#787585",
            "secondary": "#516527",
            "on-tertiary-fixed": "#00201c",
            "secondary-container": "#d3ec9e",
            "surface-variant": "#e3dffd",
            "on-error-container": "#93000a",
            "inverse-surface": "#2f2e45",
            "surface-container-high": "#e9e5ff",
            "surface-container-highest": "#e3dffd",
            "primary-fixed": "#e4dfff",
            "secondary-fixed": "#d3ec9e",
            "surface": "#fcf8ff",
            "on-secondary-fixed": "#141f00",
            "on-primary-fixed-variant": "#4130ad",
            "on-secondary-fixed-variant": "#3a4d10"
          },
          "borderRadius": {
            "DEFAULT": "0.25rem",
            "lg": "0.5rem",
            "xl": "0.75rem",
            "full": "9999px"
          },
          "spacing": {
            "margin-desktop": "3rem",
            "margin": "1rem",
            "space-xl": "2rem",
            "gutter-tablet": "1.25rem",
            "space-md": "1rem",
            "space-lg": "1.5rem",
            "margin-tablet": "2rem",
            "gutter-desktop": "1.5rem",
            "space-sm": "0.5rem",
            "gutter": "1rem",
            "space-xs": "0.25rem"
          },
          "fontFamily": {
            "headline-xl": ["Inter"],
            "headline-lg": ["Inter"],
            "label-md": ["Inter"],
            "headline-xl-mobile": ["Inter"],
            "label-sm": ["Inter"],
            "headline-lg-mobile": ["Inter"],
            "body-md": ["Inter"],

          },
          "fontSize": {

            "headline-xl-mobile": ["28px", { "lineHeight": "36px", "letterSpacing": "-0.02em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "label-sm": ["11px", { "lineHeight": "14px", "letterSpacing": "0.04em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "headline-lg-mobile": ["24px", { "lineHeight": "32px", "letterSpacing": "-0.015em", "fontWeight": "700" }],
            "body-md": ["14px", { "lineHeight": "22px", "letterSpacing": "0em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "body-lg": ["16px", { "lineHeight": "26px", "letterSpacing": "-0.005em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "body-sm": ["13px", { "lineHeight": "18px", "letterSpacing": "0.005em", "fontWeight": "400" }],
            "headline-md": ["20px", { "lineHeight": "28px", "letterSpacing": "-0.015em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "headline-sm": ["18px", { "lineHeight": "24px", "letterSpacing": "-0.01em", "fontWeight": "600" }],
            "label-lg": ["14px", { "lineHeight": "20px", "letterSpacing": "0.01em", "fontWeight": "600" }]
          }
        }
      }
    }
  </script>
<style>
    .material-symbols-outlined {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 0, 'wght' 400, 'GRAD' 0, 'opsz' 24;
      display: inline-block;
      vertical-align: middle;
      line-height: 1;
    }
    .material-symbols-outlined[data-weight="fill"] {
      font-variation-settings: 'FILL' 1;
    }
  </style>
<style>
    body {
      min-height: max(884px, 100dvh);
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body class="bg-[#F7F6FC] text-on-surface antialiased min-h-full flex flex-col justify-between selection:bg-primary-container selection:text-white">
<!-- Mobile Viewport Container -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md mx-auto flex flex-col flex-1 pb-space-xl">
<!-- Top Bar Navigation (Linear Transactional Flow, No global bottom shell needed per mandate) -->
<header class="w-full px-margin py-space-sm flex justify-between items-center sticky top-0 z-50 bg-[#F7F6FC]/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<!-- Back Chevron -->
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<!-- Secondary Action Link -->
</header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="px-margin flex-1 flex flex-col pt-space-xs">
<!-- Headline & Subtitle -->
<div class="mb-space-lg">
<!-- Minimal Band 7+ target badge -->
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-primary-container font-label-sm text-label-sm mb-space-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" data-weight="fill">school</span>
<span>IELTS Academic Prep</span>
</div>
<h1 class="font-headline-lg-mobile text-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight mb-space-xs">
          Create your account
        </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          Save your IELTS study plan and sync your progress across devices.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Social Sign-In Button -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-white border border-[#25243A]/10 hover:border-outline-variant hover:bg-surface-bright rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center gap-3 shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(37,36,58,0.04)] active:scale-[0.99] transition-all mb-space-md" type="button">
<!-- Google Logo SVG -->
<svg aria-hidden="true" class="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0" viewbox="0 0 24 24">
<path d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z" fill="#4285F4"></path>
<path d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z" fill="#34A853"></path>
<path d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.06H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.94l2.85-2.22.81-.63z" fill="#FBBC05"></path>
<path d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.06l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.52 6.16-4.52z" fill="#EA4335"></path>
</svg>
<span class="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface">Continue with Google</span>
</button>
<!-- Divider -->
<div class="relative flex items-center justify-center my-space-xs mb-space-md">
<div class="w-full border-t border-[#25243A]/10"></div>
<span class="absolute bg-[#F7F6FC] px-3 font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant/80">
          or continue with email
        </span>
</div>
<!-- Registration Form -->
<form class="space-y-space-md" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();">
<!-- Field 1: Full Name -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface" for="fullName">
            Full Name
          </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] px-4 rounded-[14px] bg-white border border-[#25243A]/12 text-on-surface placeholder:text-outline-variant font-body-md text-body-md focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary-container/15 focus:outline-none transition-all shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(37,36,58,0.03)]" id="fullName" name="fullName" placeholder="Alex Morgan" type="text"/>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Field 2: Email address with Inline Validation Error -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface" for="email">
            Email address
          </label>
<div class="relative">
<input aria-describedby="email-error" aria-invalid="true" class="w-full h-[52px] px-4 rounded-[14px] bg-[#FDF0F0] border-2 border-[#B42318] text-[#25243A] font-body-md text-body-md focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-[#B42318]/15 transition-all shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(180,35,24,0.05)]" id="email" name="email" type="email" value="alex.morgan@domain"/>
<div class="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#B42318] pointer-events-none flex items-center"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-weight="fill">error</span></div>
</div>
<!-- Inline Error Text -->
<p class="text-[#B42318] text-xs font-medium mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 tracking-tight" id="email-error"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] leading-none">warning</span><span>Please enter a valid email address (e.g. name@example.com)</span></p>
</div>
<!-- Field 3: Password with Visibility Toggle & Strength Meter -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<label class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface" for="password">
            Password
          </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] pl-4 pr-12 rounded-[14px] bg-white border border-[#25243A]/12 text-on-surface font-body-md text-body-md focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary-container/15 focus:outline-none transition-all shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(37,36,58,0.03)]" id="password" name="password" type="password" value="••••••••••"/>
<button aria-label="Toggle password visibility" class="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface p-1 rounded transition-colors" id="togglePassword" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" id="eyeIcon">visibility</span>
</button>
</div>
<!-- Password Strength Indicator -->
<div class="mt-1 space-y-1.5">
<div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 w-full">
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#38A169]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#38A169]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#38A169]"></div>
<div class="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#25243A]/10"></div>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<span class="text-xs font-medium text-[#38A169] flex items-center gap-1"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" data-weight="fill">check_circle</span>Good</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant">8+ chars required</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Terms & Privacy Checkbox -->
<div class="pt-space-xs">
<label class="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer select-none">
<div class="relative flex items-center mt-0.5">
<input class="peer sr-only" id="terms" type="checkbox"/>
<div class="w-5 h-5 rounded-md border-2 border-[#25243A]/20 bg-white flex items-center justify-center text-transparent peer-checked:bg-primary-container peer-checked:border-primary-container peer-checked:text-white peer-focus:ring-2 peer-focus:ring-primary-container/30 transition-all shadow-sm"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] font-bold">check</span></div>
</div>
<span class="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-normal">
              I agree to the <a class="text-primary-container font-semibold hover:underline" href="#terms">Terms of Service</a> and <a class="text-primary-container font-semibold hover:underline" href="#privacy">Privacy Policy</a>.
            </span>
</label>
</div>
<!-- Primary CTA Button -->
<div class="pt-space-xs">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-[#25243A]/10 text-on-surface-variant/50 font-label-lg text-label-lg font-semibold rounded-[14px] cursor-not-allowed transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 pointer-events-none" disabled="" type="submit"><span>Create Account</span><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span></button>
</div>
</form>
<!-- Bottom Note / Footer Redirect -->
<div class="mt-space-lg text-center pb-space-sm">
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
          Already have an account? 
          <a class="text-primary-container font-semibold hover:underline ml-1" href="#login">
            Log in
          </a>
</p>
</div>
</main>
</div>
<script>
    // Lightweight password visibility toggle interaction
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
    const eyeIcon = document.getElementById('eyeIcon');

    if (toggleBtn && passwordInput && eyeIcon) {
      toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isPassword = passwordInput.getAttribute('type') === 'password';
        passwordInput.setAttribute('type', isPassword ? 'text' : 'password');
        eyeIcon.textContent = isPassword ? 'visibility_off' : 'visibility';
      });
    }
  </script>
</body></html>

<!-- Mobile Viewport Container capped at max-w-md -->

<header class="flex justify-between items-center w-full py-space-sm mb-2 sticky top-0 z-40 bg-background/90 backdrop-blur-md">
<button aria-label="Go back" class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined" data-icon="arrow_back">arrow_back</span>
</button>
</header>
<!-- Main Content Area -->
<main class="flex-1 flex flex-col justify-center">
<!-- Heading Cluster -->
<div class="mt-2 mb-6">
<h1 class="font-headline-xl-mobile text-headline-xl-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight">
          Welcome back
        </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant mt-1.5">
          Log in to continue your IELTS prep sprint.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Main Surface Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-5 custom-shadow border border-slate-100 ambient-halo">
<!-- Error Alert Banner (Active Error State) -->
<div class="bg-[#FDF0F0] border border-[#E5484D]/40 rounded-[14px] p-3.5 mb-5 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-all" role="alert"><span class="material-symbols-outlined shrink-0 text-[20px]" data-icon="error" style="color: #B42318;">error</span><p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm font-medium leading-snug" style="color: #B42318;">Email or password is incorrect. Please try again.</p></div>
<!-- Social Auth: Continue with Google -->
<button class="w-full bg-surface-container-lowest hover:bg-surface-container-low active:scale-[0.99] border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] py-3.5 px-4 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 transition-all duration-150" type="button">
<svg class="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" fill="none" viewbox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<path d="M23.745 12.27c0-.7-.06-1.4-.19-2.07H12v4.51h6.6c-.29 1.52-1.14 2.82-2.4 3.68v3.05h3.88c2.27-2.09 3.66-5.17 3.66-9.17z" fill="#4285F4"></path>
<path d="M12 24c3.24 0 5.95-1.08 7.93-2.91l-3.88-3.05c-1.08.72-2.45 1.16-4.05 1.16-3.12 0-5.77-2.1-6.72-4.93H1.25v3.15C3.26 21.36 7.33 24 12 24z" fill="#34A853"></path>
<path d="M5.28 14.27c-.25-.72-.38-1.49-.38-2.27s.13-1.55.38-2.27V6.58H1.25C.45 8.17 0 9.97 0 12s.45 3.83 1.25 5.42l4.03-3.15z" fill="#FBBC05"></path>
<path d="M12 4.75c1.77 0 3.35.61 4.6 1.8l3.42-3.42C17.95 1.19 15.24 0 12 0 7.33 0 3.26 2.64 1.25 6.58l4.03 3.15c.95-2.83 3.6-4.98 6.72-4.98z" fill="#EA4335"></path>
</svg>
<span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface font-semibold">
            Continue with Google
          </span>
</button>
<!-- Divider -->
<div class="relative flex items-center justify-center my-5">
<div class="w-full border-t border-slate-200"></div>
<span class="absolute px-3 bg-surface-container-lowest font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal">
            or log in with email
          </span>
</div>
<!-- Form Fields -->
<form class="space-y-4" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();">
<!-- Email Input -->
<div class="space-y-1.5">
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant" for="email">
              Email address
            </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] px-4 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20 transition-all placeholder:text-outline" id="email" placeholder="Enter your email" type="email" value="alex.morgan@example.com"/>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant text-[20px] pointer-events-none" data-icon="mail">mail</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Password Input -->
<div class="space-y-1.5">
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant" for="password">
              Password
            </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] px-4 pr-11 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface tracking-widest focus:outline-none focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20 transition-all" id="password" type="password" value="••••••••••••"/>
<button aria-label="Toggle password visibility" class="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface p-1 rounded-md transition-colors" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="visibility">visibility</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Options Row: Remember Me & Forgot Password -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
<label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
<input checked="" class="w-4 h-4 rounded text-primary-container focus:ring-primary-container/20 border-slate-300" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Remember me</span>
</label>
<a class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-primary-container hover:text-primary font-medium transition-colors" href="#">
              Forgot password?
            </a>
</div>
<!-- Primary CTA Button (LOADING STATE) -->
<div class="pt-2">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary text-on-primary font-label-lg text-label-lg rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm active:translate-y-[1px] transition-all cursor-pointer font-semibold" type="submit">Log in</button>
</div>
</form>
</div>
<!-- Quick Band Goal Badge for Encouragement -->
</main>
<!-- Bottom Footer Anchor -->
<footer class="pt-6 pb-2 text-center">
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
        Don't have an account? 
        <a class="text-primary-container hover:text-primary font-semibold transition-colors underline-offset-4 hover:underline" href="#">
          Sign up
        </a>
</p>
</footer>
</div>
</body></html>

<path d="M12 4.75c1.77 0 3.35.61 4.6 1.8l3.42-3.42C17.95 1.19 15.24 0 12 0 7.33 0 3.26 2.64 1.25 6.58l4.03 3.15c.95-2.83 3.6-4.98 6.72-4.98z" fill="#EA4335"></path>
</svg>
<span class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-on-surface font-semibold">
            Continue with Google
          </span>
</button>
<!-- Divider -->
<div class="relative flex items-center justify-center my-5">
<div class="w-full border-t border-slate-200"></div>
<span class="absolute px-3 bg-surface-container-lowest font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant font-normal">
            or log in with email
          </span>
</div>
<!-- Form Fields -->
<form class="space-y-4" onsubmit="event.preventDefault();">
<!-- Email Input -->
<div class="space-y-1.5">
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant" for="email">
              Email address
            </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] px-4 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface focus:outline-none focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20 transition-all placeholder:text-outline" id="email" placeholder="Enter your email" type="email" value="alex.morgan@example.com"/>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant text-[20px] pointer-events-none" data-icon="mail">mail</span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Password Input -->
<div class="space-y-1.5">
<label class="block font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant" for="password">
              Password
            </label>
<div class="relative">
<input class="w-full h-[52px] bg-surface-container-lowest border border-slate-200 rounded-[14px] px-4 pr-11 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface tracking-widest focus:outline-none focus:border-primary-container focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-container/20 transition-all" id="password" type="password" value="••••••••••••"/>
<button aria-label="Toggle password visibility" class="absolute right-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface p-1 rounded-md transition-colors" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" data-icon="visibility">visibility</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Options Row: Remember Me & Forgot Password -->
<div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
<label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none">
<input checked="" class="w-4 h-4 rounded text-primary-container focus:ring-primary-container/20 border-slate-300" type="checkbox"/>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface">Remember me</span>
</label>
<a class="font-label-lg text-label-lg text-primary-container hover:text-primary font-medium transition-colors" href="#">
              Forgot password?
            </a>
</div>
<!-- Primary CTA Button (LOADING STATE) -->
<div class="pt-2">
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container text-on-primary font-label-lg text-label-lg rounded-[14px] flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 shadow-sm transition-all cursor-wait font-semibold opacity-95" disabled="" type="submit"><svg class="w-5 h-5 animate-spin text-white" fill="none" viewbox="0 0 24 24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg><span>Logging in...</span></button>
</div>
</form>
</div>
<!-- Quick Band Goal Badge for Encouragement -->
</main>
<!-- Bottom Footer Anchor -->
<footer class="pt-6 pb-2 text-center">
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
        Don't have an account? 
        <a class="text-primary-container hover:text-primary font-semibold transition-colors underline-offset-4 hover:underline" href="#">
          Sign up
        </a>
</p>
</footer>
</div>
</body></html>

<!-- Bandwise — Forgot Password (Entry) -->

<body class="bg-background text-on-surface antialiased min-h-screen flex flex-col justify-start items-center selection:bg-primary-fixed selection:text-on-primary-fixed" style="background-color: #F7F6FC;">
<!-- Mobile Container Shell -->
<div class="w-full max-w-md min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-surface relative pb-8" style="background-color: #F7F6FC;">
<!-- TopAppBar (from Shared Components JSON: docked small top app bar, back button leading, help icon trailing) -->
<header class="flex justify-between items-center w-full px-margin py-space-sm max-w-md mx-auto sticky top-0 z-50 bg-surface" style="background-color: #F7F6FC;"><button aria-label="Go back" class="p-2 -ml-2 rounded-full text-on-surface hover:bg-surface-container active:scale-95 transition-all duration-150 flex items-center justify-center" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[24px]">arrow_back</span>
</button>
<div class="w-8"></div></header>
<!-- Main Content Canvas -->
<main class="flex-1 px-margin pt-space-md flex flex-col">
<!-- Academic Goal Pill Tag -->
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 self-start px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container-high text-primary mb-3">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">lock_reset</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm tracking-wide">ACCOUNT RECOVERY</span>
</div>
<!-- Header Section -->
<div class="mb-5">
<h1 class="text-headline-lg-mobile font-headline-lg-mobile text-on-surface tracking-tight mb-2">Forgot password?</h1>
<p class="text-body-md font-body-md text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed">
          Enter the email linked to your Bandwise account and we'll send you reset instructions.
        </p>
</div>
<!-- Compact Input Field Form State (Pre-filled / Sent State) -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-[18px] p-4 shadow-sm border border-outline-variant/30 mb-4 transition-all">
<label class="block text-label-md font-label-md text-on-surface-variant mb-1.5" for="email">
          Email address
        </label>
<div class="relative flex items-center">
<input class="w-full h-[50px] px-4 rounded-[14px] bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface font-body-md border border-outline-variant/40 focus:outline-none cursor-default pr-10" id="email" placeholder="name@example.com" type="email" value="alex.morgan@example.com"/>
</div>
</div>
<!-- SUCCESS STATE CARD (Prominently displayed) -->
<!-- Action Buttons Container -->
<div class="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3">
<!-- Primary App Action CTA -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container text-on-primary rounded-[14px] font-semibold text-label-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm hover:brightness-105 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-150" type="button"><span>Send reset link</span></button>
<!-- Text Action / Secondary Escape Navigation -->
<a class="text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface font-label-lg text-body-md text-center py-2 transition-colors duration-150 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1" href="#">
<span>Back to Log in</span>
</a>
</div>
<!-- IELTS Academic Support Micro-Callout -->
<div class="mt-auto pt-6 pb-2 text-center"><p class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
Still having trouble? <a class="text-primary font-semibold hover:underline" href="#">Contact support</a>
</p></div>
</main>
</div>
<!-- Interactive Timer & Feedback Script -->
<script>
    let secondsLeft = 48;
    const countdownElement = document.getElementById('countdown-text');
    const resendBadge = document.getElementById('resend-badge');

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (secondsLeft > 0) {
        secondsLeft--;
        const formatted = secondsLeft < 10 ? `0:0${secondsLeft}` : `0:${secondsLeft}`;
        countdownElement.textContent = `Resend link in ${formatted}`;
      } else {
        clearInterval(timer);
        resendBadge.innerHTML = `
          <button type="button" onclick="triggerResend()" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-primary font-bold hover:underline">
            <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">refresh</span>
            <span>Resend reset email now</span>
          </button>
        `;
        resendBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-primary-fixed px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-on-primary-fixed transition-all cursor-pointer";
      }
    }, 1000);

    function triggerResend() {
      resendBadge.innerHTML = `
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-tertiary">check</span>
        <span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-tertiary">Email resent!</span>
      `;
      resendBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#EBF8F1] px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-[#38A169]";
    }

    function openMailClient() {
      window.location.href = "mailto:";
    }
  </script>
</body></html>

          Check your inbox
        </h2>
<p class="text-body-md font-body-md text-on-surface-variant mb-4 leading-relaxed">If an account exists for this email, we've sent a reset link to <span class="font-semibold text-on-surface">alex.morgan@example.com</span>. Check your spam folder if it doesn't arrive within 2 minutes.</p>
<!-- Divider line subtle -->
<div class="w-full h-px bg-surface-container mb-4"></div>
<!-- Resend Timer Block -->
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
<span class="text-body-sm font-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Didn't receive the email?</span>
<div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-surface-container px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" id="resend-badge">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-on-surface-variant">schedule</span>
<span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm" id="countdown-text">Resend link in 0:46</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Action Buttons Container -->
<div class="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3">
<!-- Primary App Action CTA -->
<button class="w-full h-[52px] bg-primary-container text-on-primary rounded-[14px] font-semibold text-label-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm hover:brightness-105 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all duration-150" onclick="openMailClient()" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">mail</span>
<span>Open email app</span>
</button>
<!-- Text Action / Secondary Escape Navigation -->
<a class="text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface font-label-lg text-body-md text-center py-2 transition-colors duration-150 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1" href="#">
<span>Back to Log in</span>
</a>
</div>
<!-- IELTS Academic Support Micro-Callout -->
<div class="mt-auto pt-6 pb-2 text-center">
<p class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-outline">Still having trouble? <a class="text-primary font-semibold underline underline-offset-2" href="#">Contact support</a></p>
</div>
</main>
</div>
<!-- Interactive Timer & Feedback Script -->
<script>
    let secondsLeft = 48;
    const countdownElement = document.getElementById('countdown-text');
    const resendBadge = document.getElementById('resend-badge');

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      if (secondsLeft > 0) {
        secondsLeft--;
        const formatted = secondsLeft < 10 ? `0:0${secondsLeft}` : `0:${secondsLeft}`;
        countdownElement.textContent = `Resend link in ${formatted}`;
      } else {
        clearInterval(timer);
        resendBadge.innerHTML = `
          <button type="button" onclick="triggerResend()" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-primary font-bold hover:underline">
            <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">refresh</span>
            <span>Resend reset email now</span>
          </button>
        `;
        resendBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-primary-fixed px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-on-primary-fixed transition-all cursor-pointer";
      }
    }, 1000);

    function triggerResend() {
      resendBadge.innerHTML = `
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-tertiary">check</span>
        <span class="text-label-sm font-label-sm text-tertiary">Email resent!</span>
      `;
      resendBadge.className = "inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#EBF8F1] px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-[#38A169]";
    }

    function openMailClient() {
      window.location.href = "mailto:";
    }
  </script>
</body></html>
